{% comment %}
The Enterprise price calculator dialog, opened from the Enterprise card.

Included by templates/includes/plan_cards.html so parents and teachers get the
identical dialog. Pricing and school search live in static/js/school_finder.js,
which is shared with /subscription/redeem-package; this file only owns the
dialog's open/close and focus behaviour.
{% endcomment %}

  <script src="{% static 'js/school_finder.js' %}"></script>

  <script>
  /* Dialog behaviour only. Pricing and the school search are handled by
     school_finder.js, which is shared with /subscription/redeem-package. */
  (function () {
    var dialog = document.getElementById('enterprise-pricing-dialog');
    if (!dialog) return;
    var openers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-open-enterprise-pricing]');
    var closers = dialog.querySelectorAll('[data-close-enterprise-pricing]');
    var firstField = dialog.querySelector('[data-sf-province]');
    var lastFocused = null;

    function openDialog(event) {
      if (event) event.preventDefault();
      lastFocused = document.activeElement;
      if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') dialog.showModal();
      else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
      if (firstField && typeof firstField.focus === 'function') firstField.focus();
    }

    function closeDialog() {
      if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
      else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
      if (lastFocused && typeof lastFocused.focus === 'function') lastFocused.focus();
      lastFocused = null;
    }

    openers.forEach(function (opener) {
      opener.addEventListener('click', openDialog);
    });
    closers.forEach(function (closer) {
      closer.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);
    });

    /* Clicking the backdrop closes the dialog. */
    dialog.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (event.target === dialog) closeDialog();
    });

    /* Escape closes it too, and returns focus to the control that opened it. */
    dialog.addEventListener('cancel', function (event) {
      if (event.preventDefault) event.preventDefault();
      closeDialog();
    });
    dialog.addEventListener('close', function () {
      if (lastFocused && typeof lastFocused.focus === 'function') lastFocused.focus();
      lastFocused = null;
    });
  })();
  </script>

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\includes\plan_cards.html =====

{% comment %}
The three SGILA plans, rendered identically for parents, teachers and
signed-out visitors.

Included by templates/subscription.html so no two audiences can drift apart.
Prices come from context built in lessons.pricing â€” no numbers appear here.

Set ``can_choose_plans`` to False for a read-only view: the same three cards,
with every call-to-action pointing at registration instead of a POST that
would bounce an anonymous visitor to the login page. Enterprise is always
activated with a package code on /subscription/redeem-package, so it has no
POST of its own.
{% endcomment %}
<div class="epic-cards">

  {% if can_choose_plans %}
  <form method="POST" class="epic-card epic-card-individual">
    {% csrf_token %}
    <input type="hidden" name="plan" value="individual">
    <h2 class="epic-plan-name">Individual</h2>
    <p class="epic-billed epic-billed-tight">For 1 child</p>
    <p class="epic-price">{{ plan_prices.individual }}<span>/month</span></p>
    <p class="epic-billed">Billed Monthly</p>
    <button class="epic-cta epic-cta-individual" type="submit">Choose Individual Plan</button>
  </form>
  {% else %}
  <div class="epic-card epic-card-individual">
    <h2 class="epic-plan-name">Individual</h2>
    <p class="epic-billed epic-billed-tight">For 1 child</p>
    <p class="epic-price">{{ plan_prices.individual }}<span>/month</span></p>
    <p class="epic-billed">Billed Monthly</p>
    <a class="epic-cta epic-cta-individual epic-cta-link" href="/register">Start with a free trial</a>
  </div>
  {% endif %}

  {% if can_choose_plans %}
  <form method="POST" class="epic-card epic-card-family">

    <input type="hidden" name="plan" value="family">
    <span class="epic-ribbon">Best value</span>
    <h2 class="epic-plan-name">Family</h2>
    <p class="epic-billed epic-billed-tight">For up to 4 children</p>
    <p class="epic-price">{{ plan_prices.family }}<span>/month</span></p>
    <p class="epic-billed">Billed Monthly</p>
    <button class="epic-cta epic-cta-family" type="submit">Choose Family Plan</button>
  </form>
  {% else %}
  <div class="epic-card epic-card-family">
    <span class="epic-ribbon">Best value</span>
    <h2 class="epic-plan-name">Family</h2>

{% comment %}
Shared Enterprise school finder: pick a province, search for the school, and get
its quintile and price automatically. Included by both the pricing dialog on
/subscription and /subscription/redeem-package so the two cannot drift apart.

Every price shown here comes from lessons.pricing via schools.resolution; this
template contains no numbers. Behaviour lives in static/js/school_finder.js.
{% endcomment %}
<div class="school-finder"
     data-school-finder
     data-sf-instance="{{ school_finder.instance }}"
     data-sf-search="{{ school_finder.search_endpoint }}"
     data-sf-quote="{{ school_finder.quote_endpoint }}"
     data-sf-minimum="{{ school_finder.minimum_learners }}"
     data-sf-min-chars="{{ school_finder.min_query_length }}"
     data-sf-redeem="{{ school_finder.redeem_url }}"
     {% if school_finder.autofill_name %}data-sf-autofill-name="redeem"{% endif %}
     {% if school_finder.selected_school %}
       data-sf-preselect-json="{{ school_finder.selected_school_json }}"
     {% endif %}>

  <div class="pricing-calculator-fields">
    <label class="pricing-field" for="sf-province-{{ school_finder.instance }}">
      Province
      <select id="sf-province-{{ school_finder.instance }}" data-sf-province>
        <option value="">Choose your province&hellip;</option>
        {% for province in school_finder.provinces %}
          <option value="{{ province }}">{{ province }}</option>
        {% endfor %}
      </select>
    </label>

    <div class="pricing-field">
      <label for="sf-query-{{ school_finder.instance }}">School name</label>
      <div class="sf-combobox">
        <input
          type="text"
          id="sf-query-{{ school_finder.instance }}"
          data-sf-query
          role="combobox"
          autocomplete="off"
          aria-autocomplete="list"
          aria-expanded="false"
          aria-controls="sf-list-{{ school_finder.instance }}"
          placeholder="Type at least {{ school_finder.min_query_length }} letters of your school name"
          disabled
        >
        <ul
          class="sf-suggestions"
          id="sf-list-{{ school_finder.instance }}"
          data-sf-suggestions
          role="listbox"
          aria-label="School suggestions"
          hidden
        ></ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <p class="pricing-note">
    Search your school in the Department of Basic Education's National Master List
    of Schools and we will look up its quintile and price for you. Quintiles are
    the Department of Basic Education's school poverty ranking: Quintile 1 is the
    poorest, Quintile 5 the least poor.
  </p>

  <p class="sf-status" data-sf-status role="status" hidden></p>

  <div class="pricing-result sf-selected" data-sf-selected hidden>

    </p>

    <div class="pricing-field" data-sf-manual-fields hidden>

{% extends 'base.html' %}

{% block title %}Add a seat - SGILA{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="auth-layout auth-compact">

  <aside class="auth-intro">
    <img class="auth-koaly-peek" src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt="" aria-hidden="true">
    <p class="eyebrow">{{ subscription.get_plan_type_display }} plan</p>
    <h1>Add extra active learners</h1>
    <p>
      Your plan currently supports <strong>{{ active_learner_cap }} active learners</strong>.
      Each extra seat lets one more child be active at the same time, on top of that.
    </p>
  </aside>

  <div class="form-card">
    <form method="POST" id="add-seat-form">
      {% csrf_token %}
      {% if target_child_id %}<input type="hidden" name="child_id" value="{{ target_child_id }}">{% endif %}
      <label for="seat-quantity">Number of extra seats</label>
      <input type="number" id="seat-quantity" name="quantity" min="1" max="10" value="1" inputmode="numeric">

      <div class="add-seat-price-preview">
        <span id="seat-price-line">1 seat &times; R{{ seat_price }}/month</span>
        <strong id="seat-price-total">R{{ seat_price }}/month</strong>
      </div>

      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-full" type="submit">Continue to payment</button>
      <p class="form-note subscription-back-link">
        <a href="/subscription">&larr; Back to plan</a>
      </p>
    </form>
  </div>
</section>

<script>
(function () {
  var seatPrice = {{ seat_price }};
  var input = document.getElementById('seat-quantity');
  var priceLine = document.getElementById('seat-price-line');
  var priceTotal = document.getElementById('seat-price-total');

  function update() {
    var qty = parseInt(input.value, 10);
    if (!qty || qty < 1) { qty = 1; }
    if (qty > 10) { qty = 10; }
    priceLine.textContent = qty + (qty === 1 ? ' seat' : ' seats') + ' \u00d7 R' + seatPrice + '/month';
    priceTotal.textContent = 'R' + (qty * seatPrice) + '/month';
  }

  input.addEventListener('input', update);
  update();
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

      var digits = cardNumberInput.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 19);
      cardNumberInput.value = digits.replace(/(.{4})/g, '$1 ').trim();

  // Keeps the "Messages" nav badge live on every page, so a parent/teacher
  // knows a new message arrived even if they're not on the chat screen.
  (function () {
    const badge = document.getElementById('nav-unread-badge');
    if (!badge) return;

    async function refreshUnreadBadge() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/messages/unread-count', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.unread_count > 0) {
          badge.textContent = data.unread_count;
        }
        badge.classList.toggle('is-hidden', data.unread_count <= 0);
      } catch (_) { /* ignore network hiccups, try again next interval */ }
    }

    setInterval(refreshUnreadBadge, 15000);
  })();

  <script>
  (function () {
    var activityPath = /^\/lessons\/(\d+)\/(questions|activities|visual-activity|pronunciation|spelling|vocabulary|sequencing|inference|prediction|feelings|cause-effect|theme|written-response)$/;
    var current = window.location.pathname.match(activityPath);
    if (!current) return;

    var modal = document.getElementById('activity-pause-modal');
    var pauseButton = document.getElementById('activity-pause');
    var continueButton = document.getElementById('activity-continue');
    var scoreLabel = document.getElementById('activity-pause-score');
    var closeButton = modal.querySelector('[data-close-modal]');
    var csrfToken = (document.cookie.match(/(?:^|; )csrftoken=([^;]*)/) || [])[1] || '';
    var pauseTrigger = document.createElement('button');
    pauseTrigger.type = 'button';
    pauseTrigger.className = 'btn btn-light activity-pause-button';
    pauseTrigger.textContent = 'Take a break';
    document.querySelector('.activity-top').appendChild(pauseTrigger);

    function closeModal() {
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
      modal.style.display = 'none';
    }

    async function refreshScore() {
      scoreLabel.textContent = 'Loading your score...';
      if (current[2] === 'activities' && typeof window.SgilaActivityScore === 'function') {
        var liveScore = window.SgilaActivityScore();
        if (liveScore && liveScore.total > 0) {
          scoreLabel.textContent = 'Current score: ' + liveScore.score + ' / ' + liveScore.total;
          return;
        }
      }
      try {
        var response = await fetch('/lessons/' + current[1] + '/activity-pause', {
          credentials: 'same-origin'
        });
        var data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Score unavailable.');
        scoreLabel.textContent = 'Current score: ' + data.score + ' / ' + data.total;
      } catch (error) {
        scoreLabel.textContent = 'Current score is unavailable.';
      }
    }

    function openModal() {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal.style.display = 'grid';
      continueButton.focus();
      refreshScore();
    }

    pauseTrigger.addEventListener('click', openModal);
    continueButton.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    closeButton.addEventListener('click', closeModal);

    pauseButton.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      pauseButton.disabled = true;
      pauseButton.textContent = 'Saving...';
      var resumeUrl = window.location.pathname;
      var searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      if (current[2] === 'questions' && typeof window.SgilaCurrentActivityIndex === 'function') {
        resumeUrl += '?activity_index=' + window.SgilaCurrentActivityIndex();
      } else if (searchParams.has('activity_index')) {
        resumeUrl += '?activity_index=' + searchParams.get('activity_index');
        if (current[2] === 'activities' && searchParams.has('question_index')) {
          resumeUrl += '&question_index=' + searchParams.get('question_index');
        }
      } else if (current[2] === 'activities') {
        resumeUrl += '?activity_index=0';
      }
      var score = typeof window.SgilaActivityScore === 'function'
        ? window.SgilaActivityScore()
        : null;
      try {
        var response = await fetch('/lessons/' + current[1] + '/activity-pause', {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'X-CSRFToken': decodeURIComponent(csrfToken)
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({resume_url: resumeUrl, score: score})
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not save your place.');
        window.location.href = '/grade/{{ request.session.child_grade }}?activity_menu=' + current[1];
      } catch (error) {
        pauseButton.disabled = false;
        pauseButton.textContent = 'Take a break';
        document.getElementById('activity-pause-title').textContent = 'We could not save your place';
      }
    });
  })();
  </script>
  {% endif %}
  <script>
  (function () {
    var toggle = document.getElementById('theme-toggle');
    if (!toggle) return;
    var root = document.documentElement;

    function isDark() {
      return root.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
    }

    function reflect() {
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isDark() ? 'true' : 'false');
      toggle.querySelector('.theme-toggle-label').textContent = isDark() ? 'Light Mode' : 'Dark Mode';
    }

    toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
      if (isDark()) {
        root.removeAttribute('data-theme');
        localStorage.setItem('sgila-theme', 'light');
      } else {
        root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
        localStorage.setItem('sgila-theme', 'dark');
      }
      reflect();
    });

    reflect();
  })();
  </script>
</body>

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Cause and Effect - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Cause & Effect</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Match the cause to the effect</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="matching-area" class="cause-effect-grid"></div>
  <div class="center activity-action-row">
    <button id="check-btn" class="btn btn-primary" type="button">Check my matches</button>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden activity-results-row">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/theme" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
</section><script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let causes = [], effects = [];
let userMatches = {}; // causeId â†’ effectId
let selectedCause = null;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/cause-effect`);
  const data = await res.json();
  causes = data.causes;
  effects = data.effects;
  render();
}

function render() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('matching-area');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  causes.forEach((c, i) => {
    const causeEl = document.createElement('div');
    causeEl.className = 'cause-card';
    causeEl.dataset.id = c.id;
    causeEl.textContent = c.text;
    causeEl.addEventListener('click', () => selectCause(c.id, causeEl));
    grid.appendChild(causeEl);

    const effectEl = document.createElement('div');
    effectEl.className = 'effect-card';
    effectEl.dataset.id = effects[i].id;
    effectEl.textContent = effects[i].text;
    effectEl.addEventListener('click', () => selectEffect(effects[i].id, effectEl));
    grid.appendChild(effectEl);
  });
}

function selectCause(id, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.cause-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  selectedCause = id;
}

function selectEffect(effectId, el) {
  if (selectedCause === null) {
    const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
    msg.className = 'message-area wrong';
    msg.textContent = 'Please click a cause first, then click the matching effect.';
    return;
  }
  userMatches[selectedCause] = effectId;
  document.getElementById('message-area').className = 'message-area hidden';
  // Visual feedback: show matched pair
  document.querySelectorAll('.cause-card').forEach(c => {
    if (c.dataset.id == selectedCause) c.style.opacity = '0.7';
  });
  el.style.opacity = '0.7';
  selectedCause = null;
  document.querySelectorAll('.cause-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
}

document.getElementById('check-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  let correct = 0;
  for (const [causeId, effectId] of Object.entries(userMatches)) {
    const isCorrect = String(causeId) === String(effectId);
    const causeEl = document.querySelector(`.cause-card[data-id="${causeId}"]`);
    const effectEl = document.querySelector(`.effect-card[data-id="${effectId}"]`);
    if (causeEl) causeEl.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'pair-correct' : 'pair-wrong');
    if (effectEl) effectEl.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'pair-correct' : 'pair-wrong');
    if (isCorrect) correct++;
  }
  const total = causes.length;
  const allDone = Object.keys(userMatches).length === total;
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  if (!allDone) {
    msg.className = 'message-area wrong';
    msg.textContent = `You have matched ${Object.keys(userMatches).length} of ${total}. Please match all causes first.`;
    return;
  }
  msg.className = `message-area ${correct === total ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = correct === total
    ? `Perfect! All ${total} matches are correct!`
    : `You got ${correct} out of ${total} correct. Check the highlighted pairs.`;
  setMascot(correct === total ? 'happy' : 'sad', correct === total ? 'Brilliant!' : 'Good effort!');
  document.getElementById('check-btn').disabled = true;
  document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
});

load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

    {% csrf_token %}
    <textarea
      id="chat-input"

      placeholder="Type a message about {{ child.name }}'s progressâ€¦"
      rows="1"

const CHILD_ID = {{ child.id }};
const MY_ROLE  = "{{ role }}";

const msgArea = document.getElementById('chat-messages');
msgArea.scrollTop = msgArea.scrollHeight;

const input = document.getElementById('chat-input');
input.addEventListener('input', () => {
  input.style.height = 'auto';
  input.style.height = Math.min(input.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px';
});
input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
    e.preventDefault();
    document.getElementById('chat-form').requestSubmit();
  }
});

function getCsrf() {
  const val = `; ${document.cookie}`;
  const parts = val.split('; csrftoken=');
  if (parts.length === 2) return parts.pop().split(';').shift();
  return document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value;
}

document.getElementById('chat-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const body = input.value.trim();
  if (!body) return;

  const btn = document.querySelector('.chat-send-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/messages/${CHILD_ID}/send`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCsrf() },
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      body: JSON.stringify({ body }),
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err.error || `Error ${res.status}`);
    }
    const msg = await res.json();

    appendBubble(msg.sender_name, msg.body, 'mine', formatTime(msg.sent_at));
    lastCount++; // keep the poller's count in sync so it doesn't re-add this message
    input.value = '';
    input.style.height = 'auto';
    msgArea.scrollTop = msgArea.scrollHeight;
    document.querySelector('.chat-empty')?.remove();

  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Send error:', err);
    alert('Could not send message: ' + err.message);
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false;
    input.focus();
  }
});

function appendBubble(senderName, body, side, timeStr) {
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = `chat-bubble-wrap ${side}`;
  const avatarHtml = side === 'theirs'
    ? `<div class="chat-bubble-avatar">${senderName[0].toUpperCase()}</div>` : '';
  wrap.innerHTML = `
    ${avatarHtml}
    <div class="chat-bubble-col">
      <div class="chat-bubble chat-bubble--${side}">${escHtml(body)}</div>
      <div class="chat-bubble-time ${side === 'mine' ? 'chat-bubble-time--right' : ''}">${timeStr}</div>
    </div>`;
  msgArea.appendChild(wrap);
}

function escHtml(s) {
  return s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\n/g,'<br>');
}

function formatTime(iso) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
}

// Poll for new messages every 15 seconds
let lastCount = {{ messages_list|length }};
setInterval(async () => {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/messages/${CHILD_ID}`, {
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCsrf() },
    });
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.messages.length > lastCount) {
      data.messages.slice(lastCount).forEach(msg => {
        appendBubble(msg.sender_name, msg.body,
          msg.sender_role === MY_ROLE ? 'mine' : 'theirs',
          formatTime(msg.sent_at));
      });
      lastCount = data.messages.length;
      msgArea.scrollTop = msgArea.scrollHeight;
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}, 15000);
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
(function () {
  const dialog = document.getElementById('learner-activation-dialog');
  const title = document.getElementById('learner-activation-dialog-title');
  const message = document.getElementById('learner-activation-dialog-message');
  const cancelButton = document.getElementById('learner-activation-cancel');
  const continueButton = document.getElementById('learner-activation-continue');
  let pendingForm = null;
  if (!dialog || !title || !message || !cancelButton || !continueButton) return;

  document.querySelectorAll('.learner-activation-form').forEach(function (form) {
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      pendingForm = form;
      title.textContent = form.dataset.confirmTitle || 'Please confirm';
      message.textContent = form.dataset.confirmMessage || 'Are you sure you want to continue?';
      const isBlocked = form.dataset.blocked === 'true';
      continueButton.hidden = isBlocked;
      continueButton.style.display = isBlocked ? 'none' : '';
      cancelButton.textContent = isBlocked ? 'Close' : 'Cancel';
      continueButton.classList.toggle('is-reactivate', !isBlocked && form.action.endsWith('/reactivate'));
      if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') dialog.showModal();
      else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
      cancelButton.focus();
    });
  });

  function closeDialog() {
    pendingForm = null;
    if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
    else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
  }

  cancelButton.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);
  continueButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
    const form = pendingForm;
    closeDialog();
    if (form) form.submit();
  });

  dialog.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    if (event.target === dialog) closeDialog();
  });
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\feelings.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Feelings - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Feelings</span>
  </div>
  <h1>How did they feel?</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="question-area">
    <p id="question-text" class="question-text"></p>
    <div id="options-area" class="options-grid"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="center">
    <button id="next-btn" class="btn btn-primary hidden" type="button">Next question</button>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden">
    <p>You understand feelings really well!</p>
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/cause-effect" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let questions = [], current = 0;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/feelings`);
  const data = await res.json();
  questions = data.questions;
  show();
}

function show() {
  if (current >= questions.length) {
    document.getElementById('question-area').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
    setMascot('happy', 'Wonderful!');
    return;
  }
  const q = questions[current];
  document.getElementById('question-text').textContent = `Q${current + 1}: ${q.question}`;
  document.getElementById('message-area').className = 'message-area hidden';
  document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
  setMascot('ready', 'Koaly');
  const area = document.getElementById('options-area');
  area.innerHTML = '';
  q.options.forEach(opt => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'btn btn-option';
    btn.textContent = opt;
    btn.onclick = () => checkAnswer(q.id, opt);
    area.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

async function checkAnswer(qId, selected) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-feelings-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({question_id: qId, selected_option: selected}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => {
    if (b.textContent === selected) b.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
    if (!data.correct && b.textContent === data.correct_answer) b.classList.add('choice-correct');
  });
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Correct!' : 'Keep going!');
  const btn = document.getElementById('next-btn');
  btn.textContent = current === questions.length - 1 ? 'Finish feelings' : 'Next question';
  btn.classList.remove('hidden');
}

document.getElementById('next-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { current++; show(); });
load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
const imageSets = {
  "Mandu's Secret Diary": ['g3_mandu_01.png', 'g3_mandu_02.png', 'g3_mandu_03.png', 'g3_mandu_04.png', 'g3_mandu_05.png', 'g3_mandu_06.png'],

  'The Juice Spill': ['g3_juice_01.png', 'g3_juice_02.png', 'g3_juice_01.png', 'g3_juice_02.png', 'g3_juice_01.png'],
};
const assetPath = (fileName) => `/static/img/caps/${fileName}`;
const storyboardSets = {
  "Mandu's Secret Diary": 'g3_mandu_storyboard.png',
  'Soccer Trouble': 'g3_soccer_storyboard.png',
  'The River Mistake': 'g3_river_storyboard.png',
  'Stage Fright': 'g3_stage_storyboard.png',
  'The Juice Spill': 'g3_juice_storyboard.png',
};
async function initActivities() {
const response = await fetch('{% static "data/grade3_activities.json" %}?v=storyboard-panels-20260904', { cache: 'no-store' });
if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Activity data could not be loaded.');
const payload = await response.json();
const title = document.querySelector('.grade3-activities').dataset.storyTitle;
const storyData = Object.fromEntries(payload.stories.map((story) => [story.title, story.activities]));
const data = storyData[title] || storyData["Mandu's Secret Diary"];
const storyImages = imageSets[title] || imageSets["Mandu's Secret Diary"];
const app = document.getElementById('activity-app');
document.getElementById('activity-title').textContent = title;
let voicesPromise = null;
let activeSpeechTarget = null;

function loadActivityVoices() {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return Promise.resolve([]);
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  if (voices.length) return Promise.resolve(voices);
  if (voicesPromise) return voicesPromise;
  voicesPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
    const finish = () => resolve(window.speechSynthesis.getVoices());
    window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', finish, { once: true });
    window.setTimeout(finish, 1200);
  });
  return voicesPromise;
}

function getTherapeuticVoice(voices) {
  const english = voices.filter((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith('en'));
  const preferred = ['zira', 'susan', 'aria', 'sonia', 'hazel', 'google uk english female', 'natural'];
  return english.find((voice) => preferred.some((name) => voice.name.toLowerCase().includes(name))) || english[0] || voices[0] || null;
}

function audio(label, highlightTarget = null) {
  const button = document.createElement('button');
  button.type = 'button';
  button.className = 'btn btn-light audio-assist';
  button.textContent = 'Listen';
  button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    console.log(`Playing therapeutic voiceover: ${label}`);
    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
    button.disabled = true;
    if (activeSpeechTarget) {
      activeSpeechTarget.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('is-speaking'));
    }
    activeSpeechTarget = highlightTarget;
    const voices = await loadActivityVoices();
    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(label);
    utterance.voice = getTherapeuticVoice(voices);
    utterance.lang = utterance.voice?.lang || 'en-ZA';
    utterance.rate = 0.8;
    utterance.pitch = 1.15;
    utterance.volume = 1;
    utterance.onend = () => { button.disabled = false; };
    utterance.onerror = () => { button.disabled = false; };
    const words = highlightTarget ? label.split(/(\s+)/) : [];
    if (highlightTarget) {
      let characterOffset = 0;
      highlightTarget.innerHTML = words.map((word, index) => {
        const start = characterOffset;
        characterOffset += word.length;
        return word.trim()
          ? `<span class="speech-word" data-speech-index="${index}" data-speech-start="${start}" data-speech-end="${characterOffset}">${word}</span>`
          : word;
      }).join('');
      utterance.onboundary = (event) => {
        if (event.name !== 'word') return;
        const current = event.charIndex;
        highlightTarget.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => {
          const startsAt = Number(word.dataset.speechStart);
          const endsAt = Number(word.dataset.speechEnd);
          word.classList.toggle('is-speaking', current >= startsAt && current < endsAt);
        });
      };
      const clearHighlight = () => {
        highlightTarget.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('is-speaking'));
        if (activeSpeechTarget === highlightTarget) activeSpeechTarget = null;
      };
      utterance.onstart = () => {};
      utterance.onend = () => { clearHighlight(); button.disabled = false; };
      utterance.onerror = () => { clearHighlight(); button.disabled = false; };
    }
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  });
  return button;
}

async function speakFeedback(text, feedback) {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  const voices = await loadActivityVoices();
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  utterance.voice = getTherapeuticVoice(voices);
  utterance.lang = utterance.voice?.lang || 'en-ZA';
  utterance.rate = 0.8;
  utterance.pitch = 1.15;
  utterance.volume = 1;
  feedback.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('is-speaking'));
  utterance.onboundary = (event) => {
    if (event.name !== 'word') return;
    let offset = 0;
    feedback.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => {
      const startsAt = offset;
      offset += word.textContent.length;
      word.classList.toggle('is-speaking', event.charIndex >= startsAt && event.charIndex < offset);
    });
  };
  const clearHighlight = () => feedback.querySelectorAll('.speech-word').forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('is-speaking'));
  utterance.onend = clearHighlight;
  utterance.onerror = clearHighlight;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

function feedbackMessage(score, possible) {
  if (score === possible) return { emoji: 'ðŸŽ‰', text: 'Awesome! Spot on, you got everything correct, you are a superstar! ðŸŽ‰', speechText: 'Awesome! Spot on, you got everything correct, you are a superstar!' };
  if (score === 0) return { emoji: 'ðŸ˜®â€ðŸ’¨ðŸ’ª', text: "That was tricky! Let's take a deep breath and try the whole story together!", speechText: "That was tricky! Let's take a deep breath and try the whole story together!" };
  return { emoji: 'ðŸ’›âœ¨', text: "Not quite! You're still a great reading star, we will give it another spin!", speechText: "Not quite! You're still a great reading star, we will give it another spin!" };
}

function addAnswerMark(target, correct) {
  target.querySelector('.answer-mark')?.remove();
  const mark = document.createElement('span');
  mark.className = `answer-mark ${correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  mark.textContent = correct ? 'âœ“' : 'âœ•';
  mark.setAttribute('aria-label', correct ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect');
  target.append(mark);
}

function heading(number, label, instruction) {
  const header = document.createElement('div');
  header.className = 'grade3-section-heading';
  const activityName = {
    1: 'Comprehension Questions - Remember the story',
    2: 'Sequencing - Put events in order',
    3: 'True or False - Think carefully',
    4: 'Word Detective - Find the missing words',
    5: 'Spelling Questions - Build the story words',
    6: 'Word Balloon Pop',
  }[number] || label;
  header.innerHTML = `<div class="grade3-heading-copy"><h2>Activity ${number}</h2><p>${instruction}</p></div><strong class="grade3-activity-name">${activityName}</strong>`;
  header.append(audio(instruction, header.querySelector('p')));
  return header;
}
function image(name, alt) {
  const img = document.createElement('img');
  const imageIndex = Math.abs([...name].reduce((sum, character) => sum + character.charCodeAt(0), 0)) % storyImages.length;
  img.src = assetPath(storyImages[imageIndex]); img.alt = alt || name.replaceAll('_', ' ');
  return img;
}
function section(number, label, instruction) {
  const element = document.createElement('section');
  element.className = 'grade3-activity-section';
  element.dataset.activityNumber = number;
  element.dataset.activityLabel = label;
  element.append(heading(number, label, instruction));
  app.append(element);
  return element;
}

let totalScore = {{ resume_score.score|default:0 }};
let totalPossible = {{ resume_score.total|default:0 }};
const completedActivities = new Set();
const activityScores = new Map();
const questionProgress = new Map();
let completionPayload = null;
let completionRequest = null;
const progressCopy = document.getElementById('grade3-progress-copy');
const completionCopy = document.getElementById('grade3-completion-copy');
const progressFill = document.getElementById('grade3-progress-fill');
const activityMap = document.getElementById('grade3-activity-map');
const requestedParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
const requestedActivity = Number(requestedParams.get('activity_index') || 0);
let requestedQuestion = Number(requestedParams.get('question_index') || 0);
if (!Number.isInteger(requestedQuestion) || requestedQuestion < 0) requestedQuestion = 0;

function updateQuestionResumeUrl(activity, index) {
  const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  url.searchParams.set('activity_index', String(Number(activity.dataset.activityNumber) - 1));
  if (index > 0) url.searchParams.set('question_index', String(index));
  else url.searchParams.delete('question_index');
  window.history.replaceState({}, '', url);
}

function currentScore() {
  let score = totalScore;
  let total = totalPossible;
  questionProgress.forEach((progress, activity) => {
    if (!completedActivities.has(activity)) {
      score += progress.score;
      total += progress.answered;
    }
  });
  return { score, total };
}

window.SgilaActivityScore = currentScore;

function recordPartialAnswer(activity, isCorrect) {
  const result = questionProgress.get(activity) || { answered: 0, score: 0 };
  result.answered += 1;
  result.score += Number(isCorrect);
  questionProgress.set(activity, result);
}

function saveActivityResults(payload) {
  const lessonId = document.querySelector('.grade3-activities').dataset.lessonId;
  return fetch(`/lessons/${lessonId}/activities/complete`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': '{{ csrf_token }}' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });
}

function queueActivityResults(payload) {
  completionRequest = (completionRequest || Promise.resolve())
    .catch(() => null)
    .then(() => saveActivityResults(payload));
  return completionRequest;
}

function updateProgress() {
  const total = activitySections.length;
  const currentSection = activitySections[activeActivity];
  const questionCount = currentSection
    ? currentSection.querySelectorAll('.grade3-question-card, .truth-card, .listen-spell-row, .balloon-question').length || 5
    : 5;
  const questionPosition = currentSection ? Number(currentSection.dataset.questionIndex || 0) + 1 : 1;
  progressCopy.textContent = `Activity ${activeActivity + 1} of ${total}`;
  const score = currentScore();
  completionCopy.textContent = `Question ${questionPosition} of ${questionCount} | Score ${score.score} / ${score.total}`;
  progressFill.style.setProperty('--progress', `${(completedActivities.size / total) * 100}%`);
  activityMap.innerHTML = '';
  activitySections.forEach((activity, index) => {
    const step = document.createElement('span');
    step.textContent = index + 1;
    step.setAttribute('aria-label', `Activity ${index + 1}: ${activity.dataset.activityLabel}`);
    step.classList.toggle('is-current', index === activeActivity);
    step.classList.toggle('is-done', completedActivities.has(activity));
    activityMap.append(step);
  });
}

function finishActivity(activity, score, possible, message, speakSummary = true) {
  if (completedActivities.has(activity)) return;
  completedActivities.add(activity);
  totalScore += score;
  totalPossible += possible;
  activityScores.set(activity.dataset.activityNumber, { score, possible, label: activity.dataset.activityLabel });
  const feedbackCopy = activity.dataset.activityNumber === '6'
    ? (score === possible
      ? { emoji: 'ðŸŽ‰', text: 'Yay! Spot on, superstar!', speechText: 'Yay! Spot on, superstar!' }
      : score === 0
        ? { emoji: 'ðŸŒŸ', text: "Uh-oh! Practice makes perfect. Let's try again, you can do it!", speechText: "Uh-oh! Practice makes perfect. Let's try again, you can do it!" }
        : { emoji: 'ðŸŒŸ', text: "Not quite! You're still a great reading star, give it another spin!", speechText: "Not quite! You're still a great reading star, give it another spin!" })
    : feedbackMessage(score, possible);
  const feedback = document.createElement('p');
  feedback.className = `activity-feedback ${score === possible ? 'correct' : score === 0 ? 'tricky' : 'partial'}`;
  const messageWords = feedbackCopy.text.split(/(\s+)/);
  feedback.innerHTML = `<span class="feedback-emoji" aria-hidden="true">${feedbackCopy.emoji}</span> ${messageWords.map((word, index) => word.trim()
    ? `<span class="speech-word" data-speech-index="${index}">${word}</span>`
    : word).join('')}`;
  activity.append(feedback);
  feedback.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
  if (speakSummary) speakFeedback(feedbackCopy.speechText, feedback);
  const allActivitiesCompleted = completedActivities.size === 6;
  const completedFinalActivity = activity.dataset.activityNumber === '6';
  completionPayload = {
    total_score: totalScore,
    total_possible: totalPossible,
    activity_scores: [...activityScores.entries()].map(([number, item]) => ({
      number,
      score: item.score,
      total: item.possible,
    })),
  };
  if (allActivitiesCompleted) {
    completionPayload.comprehension_score = activityScores.get('1').score + activityScores.get('3').score;
    completionPayload.visual_score = activityScores.get('2').score + activityScores.get('4').score;
    completionPayload.spelling_score = activityScores.get('5').score;
  }
  completionRequest = queueActivityResults(completionPayload);
  if (allActivitiesCompleted || completedFinalActivity) {
    const finalButton = document.getElementById('final-score-button');
    finalButton.hidden = false;
    finalButton.disabled = false;
    finalButton.textContent = 'Finish activities and view score';
    finalScorePanel.innerHTML = allActivitiesCompleted
      ? '<h2>Activities complete</h2><p>You finished all six activities. Choose below to view your score.</p>'
      : '<h2>Activity complete</h2><p>Your score is ready. Choose below to view it.</p>';
    finalScorePanel.hidden = false;
    finalButton.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  }
  updateProgress();
}

function showAnswerPrompt(question) {
  let prompt = question.querySelector(':scope > .answer-prompt');
  if (!prompt) {
    prompt = document.createElement('p');
    prompt.className = 'answer-review answer-prompt';
    prompt.setAttribute('role', 'status');
    question.append(prompt);
  }
  prompt.textContent = 'Choose an answer first, then check it.';
}

function recordQuestionAnswer(activity, question, isCorrect, correctAnswer, total, completionMessage) {
  if (question.dataset.answered === 'true') return;
  question.dataset.answered = 'true';
  question.querySelector(':scope > .answer-prompt')?.remove();
  const pagerNext = question.closest('.grade3-activity-section')?.querySelector('.question-page-navigation button:last-child');
  if (pagerNext && !pagerNext.hidden) pagerNext.disabled = false;

  recordPartialAnswer(activity, isCorrect);
  const result = questionProgress.get(activity);

  const feedback = document.createElement('p');
  feedback.className = `answer-review ${isCorrect ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  feedback.setAttribute('role', 'status');
  feedback.textContent = isCorrect
    ? 'Correct! Well done.'
    : `Not quite. The correct answer is ${String(correctAnswer).replace(/[.!?]+$/, '')}.`;
  question.append(feedback);

  if (result.answered === total) {
    finishActivity(activity, result.score, total, completionMessage, false);
  } else {
    updateProgress();
  }
  speakFeedback(feedback.textContent, feedback);
}

const visual = section(1, 'Comprehension check', 'Listen, then choose the best answer.');
const visualSelections = new Map();
data.act1_visual_mc.forEach((item, index) => {
  const card = document.createElement('article'); card.className = 'grade3-question-card';
  const question = document.createElement('div'); question.className = 'question-with-audio';
  question.innerHTML = `<span class="question-number">Question ${index + 1}</span><strong>${item.question}</strong>`; question.append(audio(item.question, question.querySelector('strong'))); card.append(question);
  const options = document.createElement('div'); options.className = 'visual-options';
  item.options.forEach((option, index) => {
    const button = document.createElement('button'); button.type = 'button'; button.className = 'text-option'; button.textContent = `${String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}) ${option.text}`;
    button.addEventListener('click', () => { if (card.dataset.answered === 'true') return; options.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((choice) => choice.classList.remove('selected')); button.classList.add('selected'); visualSelections.set(item, option); card.querySelector(':scope > .answer-prompt')?.remove(); });
    options.append(button);
  }); card.append(options);
  const check = document.createElement('button');
  check.type = 'button';
  check.className = 'btn btn-primary question-check';
  check.textContent = 'Check answer';
  check.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const choice = visualSelections.get(item);
    if (!choice) { showAnswerPrompt(card); return; }
    const correctOption = item.options.find((option) => option.isCorrect);
    const correct = Boolean(choice.isCorrect);
    options.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((button) => { button.disabled = true; });
    const selected = card.querySelector('.selected');
    if (selected) {
      selected.classList.add(correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong');
      addAnswerMark(selected, correct);
    }
    if (!correct) {
      const correctButton = [...options.querySelectorAll('button')].find((button) => button.textContent.endsWith(correctOption.text));
      correctButton?.classList.add('is-correct');
      if (correctButton) addAnswerMark(correctButton, true);
    }
    check.disabled = true;
    recordQuestionAnswer(visual, card, correct, correctOption.text, data.act1_visual_mc.length, 'Great work. You finished the comprehension questions.');
  });
  card.append(check);
  visual.append(card);
});

const sequencer = section(2, 'Story sequencer', 'Drag the events to show what happened first, next, and last.');

const cards = document.createElement('div'); cards.className = 'sequence-cards';
const shuffled = [...data.act2_sequencer].sort(() => Math.random() - .5);
function sequenceCard(item) { const card = document.createElement('article'); card.className = 'sequence-card'; card.draggable = true; card.dataset.order = item.order; const panel = item.panel || item.order; const illustration = document.createElement('span'); illustration.className = `sequence-illustration storyboard-panel-${panel}`; const illustrationImage = document.createElement('img'); illustrationImage.src = `/static/img/storyboards/${storyboardSets[title]}?v=story-events-20260904-3`; illustrationImage.alt = item.text; illustration.append(illustrationImage); const caption = document.createElement('span'); caption.className = 'sequence-caption'; caption.textContent = item.text; card.append(illustration, caption, audio(item.text, caption)); card.addEventListener('dragstart', () => card.classList.add('is-dragging')); card.addEventListener('dragend', () => card.classList.remove('is-dragging')); return card; }
for (let index = 0; index < 5; index++) { const slot = document.createElement('div'); slot.className = 'sequence-slot'; slot.dataset.order = index + 1; slot.innerHTML = `<span>${index + 1}</span>`; slot.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => event.preventDefault()); slot.addEventListener('drop', () => { const moving = document.querySelector('.is-dragging'); if (moving) slot.append(moving); }); slots.append(slot); }
shuffled.forEach((item) => cards.append(sequenceCard(item)));
const sequenceReviews = document.createElement('div'); sequenceReviews.className = 'sequence-reviews';
const sequenceCheck = document.createElement('button'); sequenceCheck.type = 'button'; sequenceCheck.className = 'btn btn-primary'; sequenceCheck.textContent = 'Check order';
sequenceCheck.addEventListener('click', () => { let score = 0; sequenceReviews.innerHTML = ''; slots.querySelectorAll('.sequence-slot').forEach((slot, index) => { const card = slot.querySelector('.sequence-card'); const correct = Boolean(card && Number(card.dataset.order) === index + 1); if (correct) score += 1; slot.classList.toggle('is-correct', correct); slot.classList.toggle('is-wrong', !correct); addAnswerMark(slot, correct); if (!correct) { const review = document.createElement('p'); review.className = 'answer-review wrong'; review.textContent = `Correct answer: ${data.act2_sequencer[index].text}`; sequenceReviews.append(review); } }); sequenceCheck.disabled = true; document.querySelectorAll('.sequence-card').forEach((card) => { card.draggable = false; card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((button) => { button.disabled = true; }); }); finishActivity(sequencer, score, 5, score === 5 ? 'Excellent order! You got them all right.' : 'Review the corrections below, then move to another activity.'); });
sequencer.append(slots, cards, sequenceCheck, sequenceReviews);

const truthSelections = new Map();
data.act3_true_false.forEach((item, index) => {
  const card = document.createElement('article');

  illustration.className = 'truth-illustration';

  line.append(audio(item.text, line.querySelector('strong')));
  card.append(illustration, line);
  const choices = document.createElement('div');
  choices.className = 'truth-choices';
  [['True', true, 'ðŸ‘'], ['False', false, 'ðŸ‘Ž']].forEach(([label, value, icon]) => {
    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = `btn ${value ? 'true-button' : 'false-button'}`;
    button.innerHTML = `<span class="truth-icon" aria-hidden="true">${icon}</span><span>${label}</span>`;
    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (completedActivities.has(truth)) return;
      choices.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((choice) => choice.classList.remove('selected'));
      button.classList.add('selected');
      truthSelections.set(item, value);
    });
    choices.append(button);
  });
  card.append(choices);
  const check = document.createElement('button');
  check.type = 'button';
  check.className = 'btn btn-primary question-check';
  check.textContent = 'Check answer';
  check.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!truthSelections.has(item)) { showAnswerPrompt(card); return; }
    const correct = truthSelections.get(item) === item.isTrue;
    card.querySelectorAll('.true-button, .false-button').forEach((button) => { button.disabled = true; });
    const selected = card.querySelector('.selected');
    if (selected) {
      selected.classList.add(correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong');
      addAnswerMark(selected, correct);
    }
    if (!correct) {
      const correctButton = card.querySelector(item.isTrue ? '.true-button' : '.false-button');
      correctButton?.classList.add('is-correct');
      if (correctButton) addAnswerMark(correctButton, true);
    }
    check.disabled = true;
    recordQuestionAnswer(truth, card, correct, item.isTrue ? 'True' : 'False', data.act3_true_false.length, 'Well done. You finished the True or False questions.');
  });
  card.append(check);
  truth.append(card);
});

const detective = section(4, 'Word detective', 'Listen, then place the right word in each blank.');
const sentence = document.createElement('div'); sentence.className = 'detective-sentence';
const blanks = {};
const detectiveResults = new Map();
data.act4_word_detective.text_segments.forEach((text, index) => {
  sentence.append(document.createTextNode(text));
  const blankData = data.act4_word_detective.blanks[index];
  if (!blankData) return;
  const question = document.createElement('span');
  question.className = 'detective-question';
  const blank = document.createElement('span');
  blank.className = `word-blank ${blankData.color}`;
  blank.textContent = '?';
  blank.dataset.id = blankData.id;
  blank.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => event.preventDefault());
  blank.addEventListener('drop', () => {
    if (question.dataset.answered === 'true') return;
    const item = document.querySelector('.word-pill.is-dragging');
    if (item) {
      blank.textContent = item.dataset.word;
      blank.classList.add('filled');
      question.querySelector('.answer-prompt')?.remove();
    }
  });
  blanks[blankData.id] = blank;
  question.append(blank);
  const check = document.createElement('button');
  check.type = 'button';
  check.className = 'btn btn-primary inline-question-check';
  check.textContent = 'Check word';
  check.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (question.dataset.answered === 'true') return;
    if (blank.textContent.trim() === '?') { showAnswerPrompt(question); return; }
    const answer = data.act4_word_detective.word_bank.find((item) => item.target_blank === blankData.id);
    const correct = Boolean(answer && blank.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() === answer.word.toLowerCase());
    question.dataset.answered = 'true';
    blank.classList.toggle('is-correct', correct);
    blank.classList.toggle('is-wrong', !correct);
    addAnswerMark(blank, correct);
    check.disabled = true;
    const feedback = document.createElement('span');
    feedback.className = `answer-review ${correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
    feedback.setAttribute('role', 'status');
    feedback.textContent = correct ? 'Correct!' : `Correct word: ${answer?.word || 'not available'}.`;
    question.append(feedback);
    speakFeedback(feedback.textContent, feedback);
    detectiveResults.set(blankData.id, correct);
    recordPartialAnswer(detective, correct);
    if (detectiveResults.size === Object.keys(blanks).length) {
      const score = [...detectiveResults.values()].filter(Boolean).length;
      bank.querySelectorAll('.word-pill').forEach((button) => { button.disabled = true; button.draggable = false; });
      finishActivity(detective, score, Object.keys(blanks).length, 'You checked every missing word. Nice work!');
    } else updateProgress();
  });
  question.append(check);
  sentence.append(question);
});

function showBalloonQuestion(index, updateUrl = true) {
  const activeIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, balloonCards.length - 1));
  balloons.dataset.questionIndex = activeIndex;
  if (updateUrl) updateQuestionResumeUrl(balloons, activeIndex);
  balloonCards.forEach((card, cardIndex) => { card.hidden = cardIndex !== activeIndex; });
  balloonCount.textContent = `Question ${activeIndex + 1} of ${balloonCards.length}`;
  const score = currentScore();
  completionCopy.textContent = `Question ${activeIndex + 1} of ${balloonCards.length} | Score ${score.score} / ${score.total}`;
  previousBalloon.disabled = activeIndex === 0;
  nextBalloon.hidden = activeIndex === balloonCards.length - 1;
  nextBalloon.disabled = balloonCards[activeIndex].dataset.answered !== 'true';
  balloonCheck.hidden = true;
  nextBalloon.onclick = () => {
    if (balloonCards[activeIndex].dataset.answered === 'true') showBalloonQuestion(activeIndex + 1);
  };
  previousBalloon.onclick = () => showBalloonQuestion(activeIndex - 1);
}
balloonNavigation.append(previousBalloon, balloonCount, nextBalloon);

balloonCheck.className = 'btn btn-primary balloon-check';

balloonCheck.addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (balloonSelections.size < balloonQuestions.length) return;
  let score = 0;
  balloonCards.forEach((card, questionIndex) => {
    const selection = balloonSelections.get(questionIndex);
    const selectedBalloon = selection.balloon;
    const correct = selection.option.isCorrect;
    selectedBalloon.classList.add('is-popped');
    selectedBalloon.classList.add(correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong');
    addAnswerMark(selectedBalloon, correct);
    if (correct) {
      score += 1;
    } else {
      const correctBalloon = card.querySelector('[data-correct="true"]');
      correctBalloon.classList.add('is-correct', 'is-popped');
      addAnswerMark(correctBalloon, true);
    }
    card.querySelectorAll('.word-balloon').forEach((button) => { button.disabled = true; });
  });
  balloonCheck.disabled = true;
  finishActivity(balloons, score, balloonQuestions.length, '');
});
balloons.append(balloonCheck);

    if (questionIndex < balloonCards.length - 1) nextBalloon.disabled = false;
  });
  card.append(check);
});

const activitySections = [...app.querySelectorAll('.grade3-activity-section')];
function setupQuestionPages(activity, selector) {
  const questions = [...activity.querySelectorAll(selector)];
  if (questions.length < 2) return;

  let currentQuestion = requestedActivity === Number(activity.dataset.activityNumber) - 1
    ? Math.min(requestedQuestion, questions.length - 1)
    : 0;
  const pager = document.createElement('div');
  pager.className = 'question-page-navigation';
  const previousQuestion = document.createElement('button');
  previousQuestion.type = 'button';
  previousQuestion.className = 'btn btn-light';
  previousQuestion.textContent = 'Previous question';
  const questionCount = document.createElement('span');
  questionCount.className = 'question-page-count';
  const nextQuestion = document.createElement('button');
  nextQuestion.type = 'button';
  nextQuestion.className = 'btn btn-primary';
  nextQuestion.textContent = 'Next question';
  pager.append(previousQuestion, questionCount, nextQuestion);

  function showQuestion(index, updateUrl = true) {
    currentQuestion = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, questions.length - 1));
    activity.dataset.questionIndex = currentQuestion;
    if (updateUrl) updateQuestionResumeUrl(activity, currentQuestion);
    questions.forEach((question, questionIndex) => {
      question.hidden = questionIndex !== currentQuestion;
    });
    questionCount.textContent = `Question ${currentQuestion + 1} of ${questions.length}`;
    previousQuestion.disabled = currentQuestion === 0;
    nextQuestion.hidden = currentQuestion === questions.length - 1;
    nextQuestion.disabled = questions[currentQuestion].dataset.answered !== 'true';
  }

  previousQuestion.addEventListener('click', () => showQuestion(currentQuestion - 1));
  nextQuestion.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (questions[currentQuestion].dataset.answered === 'true') showQuestion(currentQuestion + 1);
  });
  activity.append(pager);
  showQuestion(currentQuestion, false);
}

setupQuestionPages(activitySections[0], '.grade3-question-card');
setupQuestionPages(activitySections[2], '.truth-card');
setupQuestionPages(activitySections[4], '.listen-spell-row');
const finalScorePanel = document.createElement('div'); finalScorePanel.id = 'final-score-panel'; finalScorePanel.className = 'final-score-panel'; finalScorePanel.hidden = true;
const finalScoreButton = document.createElement('button'); finalScoreButton.id = 'final-score-button'; finalScoreButton.type = 'button'; finalScoreButton.className = 'btn btn-primary btn-large btn-full'; finalScoreButton.textContent = 'View final score'; finalScoreButton.hidden = true;

async function openFinalScoreDashboard() {
  finalScoreButton.disabled = true;
  finalScoreButton.textContent = 'Saving final score...';
  try {
    const response = await completionRequest;
    if (!response || !response.ok) throw new Error('Could not save final score.');
    const lessonId = document.querySelector('.grade3-activities')?.dataset.lessonId;
    if (lessonId) {
      window.location.assign(`/lessons/${lessonId}/results`);
    }
  } catch {
    completionRequest = null;
    finalScoreButton.disabled = false;
    finalScoreButton.textContent = 'Retry final score';
  }
}

finalScoreButton.addEventListener('click', () => openFinalScoreDashboard());
const navigation = document.createElement('div'); navigation.className = 'activity-navigation';
const previous = document.createElement('button'); previous.type = 'button'; previous.className = 'btn btn-light'; previous.textContent = 'Previous activity';
const next = document.createElement('button'); next.type = 'button'; next.className = 'btn btn-primary'; next.textContent = 'Next activity';
navigation.append(previous, next);
app.append(finalScoreButton);
app.append(finalScorePanel);
let activeActivity = 0;
function showActivity(index, preserveQuestion = false) {
  activeActivity = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, activitySections.length - 1));
  const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  url.searchParams.set('activity_index', String(activeActivity));
  if (!preserveQuestion) url.searchParams.delete('question_index');
  window.history.replaceState({}, '', url);
  activitySections.forEach((activity, activityIndex) => activity.classList.toggle('activity-current', activityIndex === activeActivity));
  previous.disabled = activeActivity === 0;
  next.disabled = activeActivity === activitySections.length - 1;
  activitySections[activeActivity].append(navigation);
  updateProgress();
  activitySections[activeActivity].scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
}
previous.addEventListener('click', () => showActivity(activeActivity - 1));
next.addEventListener('click', () => showActivity(activeActivity + 1));
if (Number.isInteger(requestedActivity) && requestedActivity >= 0 && requestedActivity < activitySections.length) {
  showActivity(requestedActivity, true);
} else {
  showActivity(0);
}
}

initActivities().catch((error) => {
  document.getElementById('activity-app').textContent = error.message;
});
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  const modal = document.getElementById('activity-picker');
  const options = document.getElementById('activity-picker-options');
  const detail = document.getElementById('activity-picker-detail');
  const selectedLessonId = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('activity_menu');

  function withReturnToMenu(url, lessonId) {
    const destination = new URL(url, window.location.origin);
    destination.searchParams.set('activity_menu', lessonId);
    return destination.pathname + destination.search;
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('.lesson-card[data-activities]').forEach((card) => {
    card.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const activities = JSON.parse(card.dataset.activities);
      options.innerHTML = '';
      if (card.dataset.resumeUrl) {
        const resume = document.createElement('a');
        resume.className = 'btn btn-primary'; resume.href = card.dataset.resumeUrl;
        resume.textContent = 'Continue where I paused'; options.appendChild(resume);
        detail.textContent = `You have a saved place in ${card.dataset.lessonTitle}. Or choose another activity.`;
      } else {
        detail.textContent = `Choose an activity from ${card.dataset.lessonTitle}.`;
      }
      activities.forEach((activity) => {
        const link = document.createElement('a'); link.className = 'btn btn-light';
        link.href = withReturnToMenu(activity.url, card.dataset.lessonId); link.textContent = activity.label; options.appendChild(link);
      });
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal.style.display = 'grid';
    });
  });
  function closePicker() {
    modal.classList.add('hidden');
    modal.style.display = 'none';
  }
  document.getElementById('activity-picker-cancel').addEventListener('click', closePicker);
  document.getElementById('activity-picker-close').addEventListener('click', closePicker);

  if (selectedLessonId) {
    const lessonCard = document.querySelector(`.lesson-card[data-lesson-id="${selectedLessonId}"]`);
    if (lessonCard) {
      history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
      lessonCard.click();
    }
  }
})();

(function () {
  function getCookie(name) {
    const match = document.cookie.match('(^|;)\\s*' + name + '\\s*=\\s*([^;]+)');
    return match ? decodeURIComponent(match.pop()) : '';
  }

  const button = document.getElementById('ai-story-btn');
  const status = document.getElementById('ai-story-status');
  let pollTimer = null;

  function pollJob(jobId) {
    fetch(`/ai-story-job/${jobId}/status`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data.status === 'done') {
          clearInterval(pollTimer);
          status.textContent = 'Your story is ready!';
          button.textContent = 'Read your new story â†’';
          button.disabled = false;
          button.onclick = () => { window.location.href = data.redirect_url; };
        } else if (data.status === 'error') {
          clearInterval(pollTimer);
          button.disabled = false;
          button.textContent = 'Explore more stories';
          status.textContent = data.error || 'Could not create a story right now. Please try again.';
        } else {
          status.textContent = 'Creating your storyâ€¦ this can take a minute.';
        }
      })
      .catch(() => { /* transient network hiccup â€” next poll will retry */ });
  }

  {% if ai_story_job_id %}
  status.textContent = 'Creating your storyâ€¦ this can take a minute.';
  pollTimer = setInterval(() => pollJob({{ ai_story_job_id }}), 3000);
  {% endif %}

  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (button.textContent.includes('Read your new story')) return;
    button.disabled = true;
    button.textContent = 'Creating your storyâ€¦';
    status.textContent = 'Starting upâ€¦';
    fetch('/grade/{{ grade }}/generate-ai-story', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCookie('csrftoken'), 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' },
    })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.success) {
          button.disabled = false;
          button.textContent = 'Explore more stories';
          status.textContent = data.error || 'Could not start your story right now.';
          if (data.redirect_url) {
            setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = data.redirect_url; }, 1500);
          }
          return;
        }
        pollTimer = setInterval(() => pollJob(data.job_id), 3000);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = 'Explore more stories';
        status.textContent = 'Something went wrong. Please try again.';
      });
  });
})();
</script>
