<script>
(() => {
  document.querySelectorAll('.help-disclosure').forEach((section) => {
    const action = section.querySelector('.help-section-action');
    const label = section.dataset.actionLabel;
    const updateAction = () => {
      action.textContent = `${section.open ? 'Hide' : 'Show'} ${label} form`;
    };
    section.addEventListener('toggle', updateAction);
    updateAction();
  });

  const articleList = document.getElementById('help-article-list');
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('help-search');
  const audienceSelect = document.getElementById('help-audience');
  const count = document.getElementById('help-result-count');
  let availableArticles = [];
  const initialParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const initialAudience = initialParams.get('audience');
  if (['parent', 'teacher', 'enterprise'].includes(initialAudience)) {
    audienceSelect.value = initialAudience;
  }
  searchInput.value = initialParams.get('q') || '';

  function topicMatchesSearch(text) {
    const terms = searchInput.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const normalizedText = text.toLocaleLowerCase();
    return terms.every((term) => normalizedText.includes(term));
  }

  function renderSearchMatches(element, text) {
    const terms = searchInput.value.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!terms.length) {
      element.textContent = text;
      return;
    }

    const normalizedText = text.toLocaleLowerCase();
    const normalizedTerms = terms.map((term) => term.toLocaleLowerCase());
    let cursor = 0;
    while (cursor < text.length) {
      let matchIndex = text.length;
      let matchedTerm = '';
      normalizedTerms.forEach((term) => {
        const index = normalizedText.indexOf(term, cursor);
        if (index !== -1 && index < matchIndex) {
          matchIndex = index;
          matchedTerm = term;
        }
      });

      if (!matchedTerm) {
        element.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(cursor)));
        break;
      }

      if (matchIndex > cursor) {
        element.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(cursor, matchIndex)));
      }
      const mark = document.createElement('mark');
      mark.className = 'help-search-match';
      mark.textContent = text.slice(matchIndex, matchIndex + matchedTerm.length);
      element.append(mark);
      cursor = matchIndex + matchedTerm.length;
    }
  }

  const categoryIconShapes = {
    'getting-started-guides': [
      ['path', { d: 'M13 4h6a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v12a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-6' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M3 21h10V3L3 5v16Z' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M7 12h.01' }],
    ],
    'account-access': [
      ['circle', { cx: '12', cy: '8', r: '4' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M5 21a7 7 0 0 1 14 0' }],
    ],
    'learning-activities': [
      ['path', { d: 'M6.5 2H20v20H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 4 19.5v-15A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 2Z' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M4 19.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 17H20M8 7h8M8 11h6' }],
    ],
    'progress-reports': [
      ['path', { d: 'M3 3v18h18' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M18 17V9M13 17V5M8 17v-3' }],
    ],
    'class-connections': [
      ['path', { d: 'M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2' }],
      ['circle', { cx: '9', cy: '7', r: '4' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M22 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-3-3.87M16 3.13a4 4 0 0 1 0 7.75' }],
    ],
    'plans-billing': [
      ['rect', { x: '2', y: '5', width: '20', height: '14', rx: '2' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M2 10h20M7 15h3' }],
    ],
    'audio-troubleshooting': [
      ['path', { d: 'M11 5 6 9H3v6h3l5 4V5Z' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M15.5 8.5a5 5 0 0 1 0 7M18.5 5.5a9 9 0 0 1 0 13' }],
    ],
    'email-delivery': [
      ['rect', { x: '3', y: '5', width: '18', height: '14', rx: '2' }],
      ['path', { d: 'm3 7 9 6 9-6' }],
    ],
    'support-feedback': [
      ['path', { d: 'M3 13v-2a9 9 0 0 1 18 0v2' }],
      ['rect', { x: '2', y: '12', width: '4', height: '7', rx: '2' }],
      ['rect', { x: '18', y: '12', width: '4', height: '7', rx: '2' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M18 19a6 6 0 0 1-6 3h-2' }],
    ],
    'enterprise-packages': [
      ['path', { d: 'M3 21h18M5 21V7l7-4 7 4v14M9 21v-5h6v5M9 9h.01M15 9h.01M9 12h.01M15 12h.01' }],
    ],
    default: [
      ['circle', { cx: '12', cy: '12', r: '9' }],
      ['path', { d: 'M9.6 9a2.5 2.5 0 1 1 4.5 1.5c-1.2 1.2-2.1 1.5-2.1 3M12 17h.01' }],
    ],
  };

  function createCategoryIcon(category) {
    const svgNamespace = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
    const svg = document.createElementNS(svgNamespace, 'svg');
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 24 24');
    svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    svg.setAttribute('focusable', 'false');
    (categoryIconShapes[category.slug] || categoryIconShapes.default).forEach(([tagName, attributes]) => {
      const shape = document.createElementNS(svgNamespace, tagName);
      Object.entries(attributes).forEach(([name, value]) => shape.setAttribute(name, value));
      svg.append(shape);
    });
    return svg;
  }

  function renderArticles(articles) {
    articleList.replaceChildren();
    const categories = new Map();
    let visibleTopicCount = 0;
    articles.forEach((article) => {
      const category = article.category;
      const categoryKey = category.slug || category.name;
      if (!categories.has(categoryKey)) {
        categories.set(categoryKey, { category, topics: [] });
      }
      const paragraphs = article.content.split(/\n{2,}/).filter(Boolean);
      const questions = [];
      paragraphs.forEach((paragraph) => {
        const questionAnswer = paragraph.match(/^Q:\s*(.*?)\s+A:\s*([\s\S]*)$/);
        if (questionAnswer) {
          questions.push({ title: questionAnswer[1], answer: questionAnswer[2] });
        }
      });
      const topics = questions.length
        ? questions
        : [{ title: article.title, answer: paragraphs.join('\n\n') }];
      topics.forEach((topic) => {
        const searchableText = `${category.name} ${topic.title} ${topic.answer}`;
        if (topicMatchesSearch(searchableText)) {
          categories.get(categoryKey).topics.push({ ...topic, article });
          visibleTopicCount += 1;
        }
      });
    });

    const visibleCategories = [...categories.values()].filter(({ topics }) => topics.length);
    if (!visibleCategories.length) {
      count.textContent = '0 topics';
      const empty = document.createElement('p');
      empty.textContent = 'No questions match your search. Try another search or send feedback below.';
      articleList.append(empty);
      return;
    }

    visibleCategories.forEach(({ category, topics }) => {
      const group = document.createElement('details');
      group.className = 'help-category-card';
      group.open = Boolean(searchInput.value.trim());
      const heading = document.createElement('summary');
      heading.className = 'help-category-heading';
      const icon = document.createElement('span');
      icon.className = 'help-category-icon';
      icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      icon.append(createCategoryIcon(category));
      const title = document.createElement('h3');
      renderSearchMatches(title, category.name);
      const description = document.createElement('p');
      description.className = 'help-category-description';
      description.textContent = category.description || 'Browse answers in this category.';
      const categoryCount = document.createElement('span');
      categoryCount.className = 'help-category-count';
      categoryCount.textContent = `${topics.length} ${topics.length === 1 ? 'topic' : 'topics'}`;
      heading.append(icon, title, description, categoryCount);
      group.append(heading);

      const topicList = document.createElement('div');
      topicList.className = 'help-category-topics';
      topics.forEach((topic, index) => {
        const question = document.createElement('details');
        question.className = 'help-question';
        if (index >= 3) question.hidden = true;
        const questionTitle = document.createElement('summary');
        renderSearchMatches(questionTitle, topic.title);
        const answer = document.createElement('div');
        answer.className = 'help-answer';
        topic.answer.split(/\n{2,}/).filter(Boolean).forEach((paragraph) => {
          const answerParagraph = document.createElement('p');
          renderSearchMatches(answerParagraph, paragraph);
          answer.append(answerParagraph);
        });
        if (topic.article.video_tutorial_url) {
          const videoLink = document.createElement('a');
          videoLink.href = topic.article.video_tutorial_url;
          videoLink.target = '_blank';
          videoLink.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
          videoLink.textContent = 'Watch the video guide';
          answer.append(videoLink);
        }
        question.append(questionTitle, answer);
        topicList.append(question);
      });
      group.append(topicList);

      if (topics.length > 3) {
        const toggle = document.createElement('button');
        toggle.className = 'help-category-toggle';
        toggle.type = 'button';
        toggle.textContent = 'See all';
        toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const expanded = toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
          [...topicList.children].forEach((topic, index) => {
            if (index >= 3) topic.hidden = expanded;
          });
          toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!expanded));
          toggle.textContent = expanded ? 'See all' : 'Show fewer';
        });
        group.append(toggle);
      }
      articleList.append(group);
    });
    count.textContent = `${visibleTopicCount} ${visibleTopicCount === 1 ? 'topic' : 'topics'}`;
  }

  async function loadArticles() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set('audience', audienceSelect.value);
    articleList.textContent = 'Loading topics...';
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/help/articles/?${params}`, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Guides could not be loaded.');
      const payload = await response.json();
      availableArticles = Array.isArray(payload) ? payload : payload.results || [];
      renderArticles(availableArticles);
    } catch (error) {
      availableArticles = [];
      articleList.textContent = error.message;
    }
  }

  audienceSelect.addEventListener('change', loadArticles);
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => renderArticles(availableArticles));
  loadArticles();

  document.getElementById('support-ticket-form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = event.currentTarget;
    const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    const status = document.getElementById('ticket-form-status');
    const formData = new FormData(form);
    const payload = Object.fromEntries(formData.entries());
    delete payload.csrfmiddlewaretoken;
    button.disabled = true;
    status.textContent = 'Sending your message...';
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/help/tickets/', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-CSRFToken': formData.get('csrfmiddlewaretoken'),
          Accept: 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(Object.values(result).flat().join(' '));
      form.reset();
      if (result.support_notification_sent && result.confirmation_email_sent) {
        status.textContent = 'Your message was sent to SGILA support. A confirmation email is on its way.';
      } else if (result.support_notification_sent) {
        status.textContent = `Your request #${result.id} reached SGILA support, but we could not send a confirmation email. Check that your email address is correct.`;
      } else if (result.confirmation_email_sent) {
        status.textContent = `A confirmation email was sent, but SGILA support could not be notified. Please email sgila.info@gmail.com and include reference #${result.id}.`;
      } else {
        status.textContent = `Your request #${result.id} was saved, but neither email could be sent. Please email sgila.info@gmail.com and include this reference.`;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      status.textContent = error.message || 'We could not send your message. Please email sgila.info@gmail.com.';
    } finally {
      button.disabled = false;
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('feedback-form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = event.currentTarget;
    const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    const status = document.getElementById('feedback-form-status');
    const formData = new FormData(form);
    const payload = Object.fromEntries(formData.entries());
    delete payload.csrfmiddlewaretoken;
    button.disabled = true;
    status.textContent = 'Sending feedback...';
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/help/feedback/', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-CSRFToken': formData.get('csrfmiddlewaretoken'),
          Accept: 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(Object.values(result).flat().join(' '));
      form.reset();
      status.textContent = result.support_notification_sent
        ? `Feedback #${result.id} was sent to the SGILA team. Thank you for helping us improve.`
        : `Feedback #${result.id} was saved, but the team could not be emailed. Please try again later.`;
    } catch (error) {
      status.textContent = error.message || 'We could not submit your feedback. Please try again.';
    } finally {
      button.disabled = false;
    }
  });
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\inference.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Inference - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Inference</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Read between the lines</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="question-area">
    <p id="question-text" class="question-text"></p>
    <div id="options-area" class="options-grid"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="center">
    <button id="next-btn" class="btn btn-primary hidden" type="button">Next question</button>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden">
    <p>Great thinking! You read between the lines.</p>
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/feelings" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let questions = [], current = 0;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/inference`);
  const data = await res.json();
  questions = data.questions;
  show();
}

function show() {
  if (current >= questions.length) {
    document.getElementById('question-area').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
    setMascot('happy', 'Brilliant!');
    return;
  }
  const q = questions[current];
  document.getElementById('question-text').textContent = `Q${current + 1}: ${q.question}`;
  document.getElementById('message-area').className = 'message-area hidden';
  document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
  setMascot('ready', 'Koaly');
  const area = document.getElementById('options-area');
  area.innerHTML = '';
  q.options.forEach(opt => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'btn btn-option';
    btn.textContent = opt;
    btn.onclick = () => checkAnswer(q.id, opt);
    area.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

async function checkAnswer(qId, selected) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-inference-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({question_id: qId, selected_option: selected}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => {
    if (b.textContent === selected) b.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
    if (!data.correct && b.textContent === data.correct_answer) b.classList.add('choice-correct');
  });
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Correct!' : 'Try again');
  const btn = document.getElementById('next-btn');
  btn.textContent = current === questions.length - 1 ? 'Finish inference' : 'Next question';
  btn.classList.remove('hidden');
}

document.getElementById('next-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { current++; show(); });
load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
(function () {
  const dialog = document.getElementById('grade-confirm-dialog');
  const message = document.getElementById('grade-confirm-dialog-message');
  const cancelButton = document.getElementById('grade-confirm-cancel');
  const continueButton = document.getElementById('grade-confirm-continue');
  let pendingForm = null;
  if (!dialog || !message || !cancelButton || !continueButton) return;

  document.querySelectorAll('.gc-confirm-inline-form, .gc-confirm-row').forEach(function (form) {
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      pendingForm = form;
      let confirmationMessage = form.dataset.confirmMessage || 'Are you sure you want to continue?';
      const gradeSelect = form.querySelector('.gc-confirm-select');
      if (gradeSelect) {
        const selectedGrade = gradeSelect.options[gradeSelect.selectedIndex].textContent;
        confirmationMessage += ': ' + selectedGrade + '?';
      }
      message.textContent = confirmationMessage;
      if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') dialog.showModal();
      else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
      continueButton.focus();
    });
  });

  function closeDialog() {
    pendingForm = null;
    if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
    else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
  }

  cancelButton.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);
  continueButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
    const form = pendingForm;
    closeDialog();
    if (form) form.submit();
  });

  dialog.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    if (event.target === dialog) closeDialog();
  });
})();
(function () {
  const dialog = document.getElementById('deactivate-dialog');
  const openButton = document.getElementById('open-deactivate-dialog');
  const closeButton = document.getElementById('close-deactivate-dialog');
  if (!dialog || !openButton || !closeButton) return;
  openButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') dialog.showModal();
    else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
  });
  closeButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
    else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
  });
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Prediction - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Prediction</span>
  </div>
  <h1>What happens next?</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="question-area">
    <div id="stop-point-box" class="stop-point-box"></div>
    <p id="question-text" class="question-text"></p>
    <div id="options-area" class="options-grid"></div>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden activity-results-row">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/feelings" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
</section><script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let questionId = null;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/prediction`);
  const data = await res.json();
  questionId = data.id;
  document.getElementById('stop-point-box').textContent = `Story stop: "${data.stop_point_text}"`;
  document.getElementById('question-text').textContent = data.question;
  const area = document.getElementById('options-area');
  area.innerHTML = '';
  data.options.forEach(opt => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'btn btn-option';
    btn.textContent = opt;
    btn.onclick = () => checkAnswer(opt);
    area.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

async function checkAnswer(selected) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-prediction-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({question_id: questionId, selected_option: selected}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => {
    if (b.textContent === selected) b.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
    if (!data.correct && b.textContent === data.correct_answer) b.classList.add('choice-correct');
  });
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Well done!' : 'Good try!');
  document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
}

load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

          {% if '/storyboards/' in word.image_url or '/vocab_sheets/' in word.image_url %}
            <div class="pronunciation-image storyboard-crop{% if '#panel-2' in word.image_url %} storyboard-panel-2{% elif '#panel-3' in word.image_url %} storyboard-panel-3{% elif '#panel-4' in word.image_url %} storyboard-panel-4{% elif '#panel-5' in word.image_url %} storyboard-panel-5{% elif '#panel-6' in word.image_url %} storyboard-panel-6{% else %} storyboard-panel-1{% endif %}">
              <img src="{{ word.image_url }}" alt="{{ word.word }}">

          <span>{{ word.isizulu_word }}</span>
        </div>

          <button type="button" class="btn btn-mint" data-audio="{{ word.isizulu_audio }}" data-label="isiZulu" data-lang="zu-ZA" data-say="{{ word.isizulu_word|default:word.word }}">isiZulu</button>
        </div>
      </article>
    {% empty %}
      <p class="empty">Pronunciation words are coming soon.</p>
    {% endfor %}
  </div>
  <p id="pronunciation-status" class="message-area hidden" role="status"></p>
  <div class="center">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/spelling" class="btn btn-primary">Spelling activity</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const pronunciationStatus = document.getElementById('pronunciation-status');
let voicesPromise = null;

function showPronunciationStatus(message, type = 'correct') {
  pronunciationStatus.className = `message-area ${type}`;
  pronunciationStatus.textContent = message;
}

function loadVoices() {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return Promise.resolve([]);
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  if (voices.length) return Promise.resolve(voices);
  if (voicesPromise) return voicesPromise;

  voicesPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
    let settled = false;
    const finish = () => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      resolve(window.speechSynthesis.getVoices());
    };
    window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', finish, { once: true });
    window.setTimeout(finish, 1200);
  });
  return voicesPromise;
}

function getNeutralVoice(lang = 'en-ZA') {
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const languagePrefix = lang.split('-')[0].toLowerCase();
  const neutralNames = ['aria', 'sonia', 'ryan', 'david', 'mark', 'zira', 'google uk english', 'google us english', 'natural'];
  return voices.find((voice) =>
    voice.lang.toLowerCase() === lang.toLowerCase() &&
    neutralNames.some((name) => voice.name.toLowerCase().includes(name))
  ) || voices.find((voice) =>
    voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(languagePrefix) &&
    neutralNames.some((name) => voice.name.toLowerCase().includes(name))
  ) || voices.find((voice) =>
    voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(languagePrefix)
  ) || voices.find((voice) =>
    voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith('en')
  ) || voices[0] || null;
}

async function speakWord(button) {
  const originalLabel = button.dataset.label || button.textContent.trim();
  const text = button.dataset.say || originalLabel;
  const lang = button.dataset.lang || 'en-ZA';

  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
    showPronunciationStatus('Speech is not available in this browser. Try opening the app in Microsoft Edge or Chrome.', 'wrong');
    return;
  }

  button.disabled = true;
  button.textContent = 'Loading voice...';
  await loadVoices();

  const voice = getNeutralVoice(lang);
  if (!voice) {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.textContent = originalLabel;
    showPronunciationStatus('No browser voices were found. Install a Windows speech voice or try another browser.', 'wrong');
    return;
  }

  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  window.speechSynthesis.resume();

  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  utterance.voice = voice;
  utterance.lang = voice.lang || lang;
  utterance.rate = 0.9;
  utterance.pitch = 1;
  utterance.volume = 1;

  let started = false;
  button.textContent = 'Playing...';
  showPronunciationStatus(`${originalLabel}: ${text}`, 'correct');
  utterance.onstart = () => {
    started = true;
    button.textContent = 'Playing...';
    showPronunciationStatus(`${originalLabel}: ${text}`, 'correct');
  };
  utterance.onend = () => {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.textContent = `Replay ${originalLabel}`;
  };
  utterance.onerror = () => {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.textContent = originalLabel;
    showPronunciationStatus('The voice could not start. Check browser sound settings, then try again.', 'wrong');
  };

  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  window.speechSynthesis.resume();

  window.setTimeout(() => {
    if (!started && !window.speechSynthesis.speaking && !window.speechSynthesis.pending) {
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = originalLabel;
      showPronunciationStatus('The voice did not start. Check that this browser has text-to-speech voices enabled.', 'wrong');
    }
  }, 1400);
}

loadVoices();

document.querySelectorAll('[data-audio]').forEach((button) => {
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    speakWord(button);
  });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

  sequencing: 'Comprehension',
  inference: 'Comprehension',
  vocabulary_in_context: 'Vocabulary',
  prediction: 'Comprehension',
  summarising: 'Comprehension',
  character_motivation: 'Comprehension',
  text_to_self: 'Comprehension',
  fact_vs_opinion: 'Comprehension',

  visual_literacy: 'Visual matching',
  reading: 'Reading',
  emotional_literacy: 'Comprehension',
  cause_effect: 'Comprehension',

const KOALY_IMAGES = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad: "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
