let activities = [];
let activityGroups = [];
let current = Number(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('activity_index') || 0);
if (!Number.isInteger(current) || current < 0) current = 0;
let score = 0;
let completedCount = 0;
const nextButton = document.getElementById('next-question-btn');
const mascot = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
const messageArea = document.getElementById('message-area');
const responseArea = document.getElementById('response-area');
let currentMatches = {};
let activePromptKey = null;

window.SgilaCurrentActivityIndex = () => current;

function setMascot(reaction, label) {
  mascot.className = `activity-coach koaly-${reaction}`;
  mascot.querySelector('img').src = KOALY_IMAGES[reaction] || KOALY_IMAGES.ready;
  mascot.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

function activityTitle(activity, group) {
  const criterion = assessmentLabels[activity?.skill] || (group?.title || 'Story Activity').replace(/\s+Questions?$/i, '').trim();
  const format = activityTypeLabels[activity.activity_type] || 'Activity';
  return `${criterion} - ${format}`;
}

function saveScore() {
  const existing = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '{}');
  existing.comprehension_score = score;
  existing.comprehension_total = activities.length || totalQuestions;
  sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(existing));
}

function buildActivityMap() {
  const map = document.getElementById('activity-map');
  map.innerHTML = '';
  activityGroups.forEach((group) => {
    const step = document.createElement('button');
    step.type = 'button';
    step.className = 'activity-map-step';
    step.textContent = group.number;
    const firstActivity = activities[group.indexes[0]];
    step.setAttribute('aria-label', `Activity ${group.number}: ${activityTitle(firstActivity, group)}`);
    step.addEventListener('click', () => {
      current = group.indexes[0];
      document.getElementById('question-area').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('results-area').classList.add('hidden');
      renderActivity();
    });
    if (group.indexes.every((index) => index < completedCount)) step.classList.add('is-done');
    if (group.indexes.includes(current) && current < activities.length) step.classList.add('is-current');
    map.appendChild(step);
  });
}

function updateProgress() {
  const total = activities.length;
  const group = activityGroups.find((item) => item.indexes.includes(current));
  const groupPosition = group ? activityGroups.indexOf(group) + 1 : activityGroups.length;
  document.getElementById('progress-copy').textContent = group
    ? `Activity ${groupPosition} of ${activityGroups.length}`
    : (total ? 'Activities complete' : 'No activities yet');
  if (group && group.indexes.length > 1) {
    const questionPosition = group.indexes.indexOf(current) + 1;
    document.getElementById('completion-copy').textContent = `Question ${questionPosition}/${group.indexes.length}`;
  } else {
    const completeGroups = activityGroups.filter((item) => item.indexes.every((index) => index < completedCount)).length;
    document.getElementById('completion-copy').textContent = `${completeGroups} of ${activityGroups.length} activities complete`;
  }
  document.getElementById('progress-fill').style.setProperty(
    '--progress',
    total ? `${(completedCount / total) * 100}%` : '0%',
  );
  buildActivityMap();
}

function makeSubmitButton(label) {
  const button = document.createElement('button');
  button.type = 'button';
  button.className = 'btn btn-primary response-submit';
  button.textContent = label;
  return button;
}

function activityConfig(activity) {
  return activity.options && !Array.isArray(activity.options) ? activity.options : {};
}

function choiceItems(activity) {
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  return Array.isArray(activity.options) ? activity.options : (config.choices || []);
}

function visualPanelClass(imageUrl) {
  const match = String(imageUrl || '').match(/#panel-([1-6])/);
  return match ? ` storyboard-panel-${match[1]}` : '';
}

function createActivityVisual(imageUrl, alt, modifier = '') {
  const frame = document.createElement('div');
  const isSheet = imageUrl.includes('/storyboards/') || imageUrl.includes('/vocab_sheets/');
  frame.className = `activity-visual${isSheet ? ` storyboard-crop${visualPanelClass(imageUrl)}` : ''}${modifier ? ` ${modifier}` : ''}`;
  const image = document.createElement('img');
  image.src = imageUrl;
  image.alt = alt || 'Story picture';
  frame.appendChild(image);
  return frame;
}

function prepareActivitySpeechWords(elements) {
  if (!elements) return [];
  if (!Array.isArray(elements)) elements = [elements];
  const allWords = [];
  let globalCharIndex = 0;

  elements.forEach((element) => {
    if (!element) return;
    const rawText = element.dataset.speechText || element.textContent.trim();
    element.dataset.speechText = rawText;
    element.innerHTML = '';
    rawText.split(/(\s+)/).forEach((token) => {
      if (!token) return;
      if (/^\s+$/.test(token)) {
        element.appendChild(document.createTextNode(token));
      } else {
        const word = document.createElement('span');
        word.className = 'activity-speech-word';
        word.dataset.start = globalCharIndex;
        word.dataset.end = globalCharIndex + token.length;
        word.textContent = token;
        element.appendChild(word);
        allWords.push(word);
      }
      globalCharIndex += token.length;
    });
  });

  return allWords;
}

function clearActivitySpeechWords(words) {
  words.forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('spoken-word', 'current-word'));
}

function highlightActivityWord(words, characterIndex) {
  const currentWord = words.find((word) => (
    Number(word.dataset.start) <= characterIndex
    && characterIndex < Number(word.dataset.end)
  ));
  if (!currentWord) return;
  words.forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('current-word'));
  currentWord.classList.add('spoken-word', 'current-word');
}

function speakActivityText(text, button, highlightElements = null) {
  if (!text || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  const elements = highlightElements || [];
  const words = prepareActivitySpeechWords(elements);
  clearActivitySpeechWords(words);
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  utterance.voice = voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase() === 'en-za')
    || voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith('en'))
    || null;
  utterance.lang = utterance.voice ? utterance.voice.lang : 'en-ZA';
  utterance.rate = 0.86;
  utterance.pitch = 1;
  utterance.onboundary = (event) => {
    if (!event.name || event.name === 'word') {
      highlightActivityWord(words, event.charIndex);
    }
  };
  if (button) {
    button.disabled = true;
    button.textContent = 'Listening...';
    utterance.onend = () => {
      words.forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('current-word'));
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = 'Listen again';
    };
    utterance.onerror = () => {
      clearActivitySpeechWords(words);
      utterance.onend();
    };
  }
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

function renderActivityMedia(activity, questionText, instructionText) {
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  if (!config.prompt_image && !config.audio_text && !config.focus_word) return;

  const media = document.createElement('div');
  media.className = 'activity-media';
  if (config.presentation === 'grade1_phonics') media.classList.add('grade1-phonics-media');
  if (!config.prompt_image) media.classList.add('audio-only');
  if (config.prompt_image) {
    media.appendChild(createActivityVisual(
      config.prompt_image,
      config.prompt_alt || config.focus_word || 'Story clue',
      config.prompt_image_mode === 'silhouette' ? 'is-silhouette' : '',
    ));
  }

  const actions = document.createElement('div');
  actions.className = 'activity-media-copy';
  if (config.display_word) {
    const displayWord = document.createElement('strong');
    displayWord.className = 'grade1-phonics-word';
    const wordTemplate = String(config.display_word);
    const blankIndex = wordTemplate.indexOf('_');
    if (blankIndex === -1) {
      displayWord.textContent = wordTemplate;
    } else {
      const blank = document.createElement('span');
      blank.className = 'grade1-phonics-blank';
      blank.setAttribute('aria-label', 'missing letter');
      blank.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      displayWord.append(
        document.createTextNode(wordTemplate.slice(0, blankIndex)),
        blank,
        document.createTextNode(wordTemplate.slice(blankIndex + 1)),
      );
      displayWord.setAttribute('aria-label', `Incomplete word: ${wordTemplate.replace('_', 'blank')}`);
    }
    actions.appendChild(displayWord);
  }
  if (config.focus_word) {
    const word = document.createElement('strong');
    word.className = 'pronunciation-focus-word';
    word.textContent = config.focus_word;
    actions.appendChild(word);
    if (config.translation) {
      const translation = document.createElement('span');
      translation.className = 'pronunciation-translation';
      translation.textContent = config.translation;
      actions.appendChild(translation);
    }
  }
  if (config.audio_text) {
    const listen = document.createElement('button');
    listen.type = 'button';
    listen.className = 'btn btn-light activity-listen';
    listen.textContent = 'Listen';
    const isPhonicsWord = config.presentation === 'grade1_phonics';
    if (isPhonicsWord) {
      const spokenText = config.audio_text;
      listen.addEventListener('click', () => speakActivityText(
        spokenText,
        listen,
        null,
      ));
    } else {
      const instructionEl = document.getElementById('activity-instruction');
      const instruction = instructionText || typeInstructions[activity.activity_type] || '';
      const question = activity.question;
      const spokenText = instruction;
      const highlightElements = instructionEl ? [instructionEl, questionText] : [questionText];
      listen.addEventListener('click', () => speakActivityText(
        spokenText,
        listen,
        highlightElements,
      ));
    }
    actions.appendChild(listen);
  }
  media.appendChild(actions);
  responseArea.appendChild(media);
}

function renderChoice(activity) {
  const grid = document.createElement('div');
  const options = choiceItems(activity);
  const hasPictures = options.some((option) => option && typeof option === 'object' && option.image_url);
  const hideOptionMarkers = activity.group_title === 'Short Sentences & Picture Matching';
  const hideGradeFourChoiceMarkers = Number('{{ lesson.grade }}') === 4
    && ['multiple_choice', 'true_false'].includes(activity.activity_type);
  grid.className = `options-grid${hasPictures ? ' visual-options-grid' : ''}`;
  if (hideOptionMarkers || hideGradeFourChoiceMarkers) grid.classList.add('without-option-markers');
  let selectedAnswer = '';
  let selectedButton = null;

  options.forEach((option, index) => {
    const value = option && typeof option === 'object' ? option.value : option;
    const label = option && typeof option === 'object' ? (option.label || option.value) : option;
    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.className = 'btn btn-option';
    button.type = 'button';
    button.dataset.answer = value;
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');

    if (!hideOptionMarkers && !hideGradeFourChoiceMarkers) {
      const marker = document.createElement('span');
      marker.className = 'option-marker';
      marker.textContent = String.fromCharCode(65 + index);
      button.appendChild(marker);
    }
    if (option && typeof option === 'object' && option.image_url) {
      button.prepend(createActivityVisual(
        option.image_url,
        option.alt || label,
        option.image_mode === 'silhouette' ? 'is-silhouette' : '',
      ));
    }
    const copy = document.createElement('span');
    copy.className = 'option-copy';
    copy.textContent = label;
    button.appendChild(copy);

    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      grid.querySelectorAll('.btn-option').forEach((choice) => {
        choice.classList.remove('is-selected');
        choice.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
      });
      button.classList.add('is-selected');
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
      selectedAnswer = value;
      selectedButton = button;
      submit.disabled = false;
      if (activityConfig(activity).presentation === 'grade1_vocabulary') {
        speakActivityText(String(label), null);
      }
    });
    grid.appendChild(button);
  });

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check answer');
  submit.disabled = true;
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => submitActivity(activity, selectedAnswer, selectedButton));
  responseArea.append(grid, submit);
}

function renderCloze(activity) {
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  const phonicsBlank = config.presentation === 'grade1_phonics'
    ? responseArea.querySelector('.grade1-phonics-blank')
    : null;
  const phonicsWord = phonicsBlank ? phonicsBlank.closest('.grade1-phonics-word') : null;
  const bank = document.createElement('div');
  bank.className = 'cloze-word-bank';
  bank.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Word bank');
  let selectedAnswer = '';
  let selectedButton = null;

  choiceItems(activity).forEach((word) => {
    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'cloze-chip';
    button.textContent = word;
    button.dataset.answer = word;
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      bank.querySelectorAll('.cloze-chip').forEach((chip) => {
        chip.classList.remove('is-selected');
        chip.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
      });
      button.classList.add('is-selected');
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
      selectedAnswer = word;
      selectedButton = button;
      if (phonicsBlank) {
        phonicsBlank.textContent = word;
        phonicsBlank.classList.add('has-letter');
        phonicsWord.setAttribute(
          'aria-label',
          `Completed word: ${String(config.display_word).replace('_', word)}`,
        );
      }
      submit.disabled = false;
    });
    bank.appendChild(button);
  });

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check the word');
  submit.disabled = true;
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => submitActivity(activity, selectedAnswer, selectedButton));
  responseArea.append(bank, submit);
}

function renderSequence(activity) {
  const list = document.createElement('ol');
  list.className = 'sequence-list';
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  const itemImages = config.item_images || {};
  let draggedRow = null;

  activity.items.forEach((item) => {
    const row = document.createElement('li');
    row.draggable = true;
    row.classList.add('sequence-draggable');
    row.setAttribute('aria-label', `Drag to reorder: ${item}`);
    if (itemImages[item]) {
      row.classList.add('has-picture');
      row.appendChild(createActivityVisual(itemImages[item], item));
    }
    const text = document.createElement('span');
    text.className = 'sequence-copy';
    text.textContent = item;
    const controls = document.createElement('div');
    controls.className = 'sequence-controls';

    row.addEventListener('dragstart', (event) => {
      draggedRow = row;
      row.classList.add('is-dragging');
      event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', item);
    });

    row.addEventListener('dragend', () => {
      draggedRow = null;
      list.querySelectorAll('li').forEach((entry) => {
        entry.classList.remove('is-dragging', 'drag-over');
      });
    });

    row.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (draggedRow && draggedRow !== row) row.classList.add('drag-over');
    });

    row.addEventListener('dragleave', () => row.classList.remove('drag-over'));

    row.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      row.classList.remove('drag-over');
      if (!draggedRow || draggedRow === row) return;
      const rows = [...list.children];
      const draggedIndex = rows.indexOf(draggedRow);
      const targetIndex = rows.indexOf(row);
      if (draggedIndex < targetIndex) list.insertBefore(draggedRow, row.nextSibling);
      else list.insertBefore(draggedRow, row);
    });

    const up = document.createElement('button');
    up.type = 'button';
    up.className = 'sequence-move';
    up.textContent = '\u2191';
    up.title = 'Move earlier';
    up.setAttribute('aria-label', `Move ${item} earlier`);
    up.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const previous = row.previousElementSibling;
      if (previous) list.insertBefore(row, previous);
    });

    const down = document.createElement('button');
    down.type = 'button';
    down.className = 'sequence-move';
    down.textContent = '\u2193';
    down.title = 'Move later';
    down.setAttribute('aria-label', `Move ${item} later`);
    down.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const next = row.nextElementSibling;
      if (next) list.insertBefore(next, row);
    });

    controls.append(up, down);
    row.append(text, controls);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check the order');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const answer = [...list.querySelectorAll('.sequence-copy')].map((item) => item.textContent);
    submitActivity(activity, answer);
  });
  responseArea.append(list, submit);
}

function renderCrossword(activity) {
  const config = activity.options || {};
  const entries = config.entries || [];
  const rows = Number(config.rows || 0);
  const columns = Number(config.columns || 0);
  const cells = new Map();

  entries.forEach((entry) => {
    for (let offset = 0; offset < entry.length; offset++) {
      const row = entry.row + (entry.direction === 'Down' ? offset : 0);
      const column = entry.column + (entry.direction === 'Across' ? offset : 0);
      const key = `${row}-${column}`;
      const cell = cells.get(key) || {row, column, numbers: []};
      if (offset === 0 && !cell.numbers.includes(entry.number)) cell.numbers.push(entry.number);
      cells.set(key, cell);
    }
  });

  const layout = document.createElement('div');
  layout.className = 'crossword-layout';
  const grid = document.createElement('div');
  grid.className = 'crossword-grid';
  grid.style.setProperty('--crossword-columns', columns);
  grid.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Crossword answer grid');

  const focusNextCrosswordCell = (input) => {
    const cells = [...grid.querySelectorAll('input[data-cell]')];
    const nextCell = cells[cells.indexOf(input) + 1];
    if (nextCell) nextCell.focus();
  };

  const focusCrosswordCell = (input, rowStep, columnStep) => {
    const [row, column] = input.dataset.cell.split('-').map(Number);
    const candidates = [...grid.querySelectorAll('input[data-cell]')]
      .map((cell) => ({
        cell,
        row: Number(cell.dataset.cell.split('-')[0]),
        column: Number(cell.dataset.cell.split('-')[1]),
      }))
      .filter((candidate) => (
        (rowStep && candidate.column === column && (candidate.row - row) * rowStep > 0)
        || (columnStep && candidate.row === row && (candidate.column - column) * columnStep > 0)
      ))
      .sort((first, second) => {
        const firstDistance = Math.abs(first.row - row) + Math.abs(first.column - column);
        const secondDistance = Math.abs(second.row - row) + Math.abs(second.column - column);
        return firstDistance - secondDistance;
      });
    if (candidates[0]) candidates[0].cell.focus();
  };

  for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
    for (let column = 0; column < columns; column++) {
      const key = `${row}-${column}`;
      const data = cells.get(key);
      const cell = document.createElement('div');
      cell.className = data ? 'crossword-cell' : 'crossword-block';
      if (data) {
        if (data.numbers.length) {
          const number = document.createElement('span');
          number.textContent = data.numbers.join('/');
          cell.appendChild(number);
        }
        const input = document.createElement('input');
        input.maxLength = 1;
        input.autocomplete = 'off';
        input.dataset.cell = key;
        input.setAttribute('aria-label', `Crossword row ${row + 1}, column ${column + 1}`);
        input.addEventListener('input', () => {
          input.value = input.value.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, '').toUpperCase();
          if (input.value) focusNextCrosswordCell(input);
        });
        input.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
          const directions = {
            ArrowUp: [-1, 0],
            ArrowDown: [1, 0],
            ArrowLeft: [0, -1],
            ArrowRight: [0, 1],
          };
          const direction = directions[event.key];
          if (!direction) return;
          const [rowStep, columnStep] = direction;
          const previousFocus = document.activeElement;
          focusCrosswordCell(input, rowStep, columnStep);
          if (document.activeElement !== previousFocus) event.preventDefault();
        });
        cell.appendChild(input);
      }
      grid.appendChild(cell);
    }
  }

  const clues = document.createElement('div');
  clues.className = 'crossword-clues';
  ['Across', 'Down'].forEach((direction) => {
    const section = document.createElement('section');
    const heading = document.createElement('h3');
    heading.textContent = direction;
    section.appendChild(heading);
    entries.filter((entry) => entry.direction === direction).forEach((entry) => {
      const clue = document.createElement('p');
      clue.innerHTML = `<strong>${entry.number}.</strong> ${entry.clue} <small>(${entry.length} letters)</small>`;
      section.appendChild(clue);
    });
    clues.appendChild(section);
  });
  layout.append(grid, clues);

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check crossword');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const answer = {};
    entries.forEach((entry) => {
      let word = '';
      for (let offset = 0; offset < entry.length; offset++) {
        const row = entry.row + (entry.direction === 'Down' ? offset : 0);
        const column = entry.column + (entry.direction === 'Across' ? offset : 0);
        word += grid.querySelector(`[data-cell="${row}-${column}"]`).value;
      }
      answer[entry.key] = word;
    });
    submitActivity(activity, answer);
  });
  responseArea.append(layout, submit);
}

function renderDragMatch(activity) {
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  const board = document.createElement('div');
  board.className = 'drag-match-board';
  const tray = document.createElement('div');
  tray.className = 'drag-match-tray';
  const targets = document.createElement('div');
  targets.className = 'drag-match-targets';
  const assignments = {};
  let selectedPrompt = '';

  function updateSubmit() {
    submit.disabled = Object.keys(assignments).length !== (config.prompts || []).length;
  }

  function assign(promptKey, choiceKey) {
    if (!promptKey || !choiceKey) return;
    Object.keys(assignments).forEach((key) => {
      if (assignments[key] === choiceKey) delete assignments[key];
    });
    assignments[promptKey] = choiceKey;
    tray.querySelectorAll('.drag-match-chip').forEach((chip) => {
      chip.classList.toggle('is-matched', Boolean(assignments[chip.dataset.prompt]));
      chip.classList.remove('is-selected');
    });
    targets.querySelectorAll('.drag-match-target').forEach((target) => {
      const prompt = Object.keys(assignments).find((key) => assignments[key] === target.dataset.choice) || '';
      target.dataset.prompt = prompt;
      const promptData = (config.prompts || []).find((item) => item.key === prompt);
      target.querySelector('.drag-match-slot').textContent = promptData ? promptData.text : 'Drop a picture here';
      target.classList.toggle('has-match', Boolean(prompt));
    });
    selectedPrompt = '';
    updateSubmit();
  }

  (config.prompts || []).forEach((prompt) => {
    const chip = document.createElement('button');
    chip.type = 'button';
    chip.className = 'drag-match-chip';
    chip.dataset.prompt = prompt.key;
    chip.draggable = true;
    if (prompt.image_url) chip.appendChild(createActivityVisual(prompt.image_url, prompt.text));
    const label = document.createElement('span');
    label.textContent = prompt.text;
    chip.appendChild(label);
    chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
      selectedPrompt = prompt.key;
      tray.querySelectorAll('.drag-match-chip').forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('is-selected', item === chip));
    });
    chip.addEventListener('dragstart', (event) => event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', prompt.key));
    tray.appendChild(chip);
  });

  (config.choices || []).forEach((choice) => {
    const target = document.createElement('button');
    target.type = 'button';
    target.className = 'drag-match-target';
    target.dataset.choice = choice.key;
    if (choice.image_url) target.appendChild(createActivityVisual(choice.image_url, choice.text));
    const label = document.createElement('strong');
    label.textContent = choice.text;
    const slot = document.createElement('span');
    slot.className = 'drag-match-slot';
    slot.textContent = 'Drop a picture here';
    target.append(label, slot);
    target.addEventListener('click', () => assign(selectedPrompt, choice.key));
    target.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => event.preventDefault());
    target.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      assign(event.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'), choice.key);
    });
    targets.appendChild(target);
  });

  const trayTitle = document.createElement('strong');
  trayTitle.className = 'drag-match-heading';
  trayTitle.textContent = 'Pictures';
  tray.prepend(trayTitle);
  const targetTitle = document.createElement('strong');
  targetTitle.className = 'drag-match-heading';
  targetTitle.textContent = 'Story places';
  targets.prepend(targetTitle);
  board.append(tray, targets);

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check matches');
  submit.disabled = true;
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => submitActivity(activity, assignments));
  responseArea.append(board, submit);
}

function renderMatching(activity) {
  const config = activity.options || {};
  const isGradeFourMatching = Number('{{ lesson.grade }}') === 4
    && Array.isArray(config.prompts)
    && Array.isArray(config.choices);

  if (config.presentation === 'drag_match') {
    renderDragMatch(activity);
    return;
  }

  if (isGradeFourMatching) {
    const promptColors = ['blue', 'orange', 'yellow', 'purple'];
    const currentMatches = {};
    let activePromptKey = null;

    const board = document.createElement('div');
    board.className = 'matching-board';

    const promptsCol = document.createElement('div');
    promptsCol.className = 'matching-prompt-cards';

    const choicesCol = document.createElement('div');
    choicesCol.className = 'matching-choice-cards';

    const promptCards = {};
    const choiceCards = {};

    function updateMatchStyles() {
      Object.entries(promptCards).forEach(([promptKey, card]) => {
        const isSelected = activePromptKey === promptKey;
        const isMatched = Boolean(currentMatches[promptKey]);
        card.classList.toggle('is-active', isSelected);
        card.classList.toggle('is-matched', isMatched);
        if (isMatched) {
          card.style.borderColor = card.dataset.color || 'blue';
          card.style.backgroundColor = `${card.dataset.color || 'blue'}18`;
        } else {
          card.style.borderColor = card.dataset.color || 'blue';
          card.style.backgroundColor = '';
        }
      });

      Object.entries(choiceCards).forEach(([choiceKey, card]) => {
        const matchedPrompt = Object.keys(currentMatches).find((promptKey) => currentMatches[promptKey] === choiceKey);
        const isMatched = Boolean(matchedPrompt);
        card.classList.toggle('is-matched', isMatched);
        if (isMatched) {
          const promptCard = promptCards[matchedPrompt];
          const color = promptCard ? promptCard.dataset.color : 'blue';
          card.style.borderColor = color;
          card.style.backgroundColor = `${color}18`;
        } else {
          card.style.borderColor = '';
          card.style.backgroundColor = '';
        }
      });
    }

    (config.prompts || []).forEach((prompt, promptIndex) => {
      const color = promptColors[promptIndex % promptColors.length];
      const card = document.createElement('button');
      card.type = 'button';
      card.className = 'matching-prompt-card';
      card.dataset.prompt = prompt.key;
      card.dataset.color = color;
      card.setAttribute('aria-label', `Prompt: ${prompt.text}`);
      card.style.borderColor = color;
      card.textContent = prompt.text;
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (activePromptKey === prompt.key) {
          activePromptKey = null;
        } else {
          activePromptKey = prompt.key;
        }
        updateMatchStyles();
      });
      promptCards[prompt.key] = card;
      promptsCol.appendChild(card);
    });

    (config.choices || []).forEach((choice) => {
      const card = document.createElement('button');
      card.type = 'button';
      card.className = 'matching-choice-card';
      card.dataset.choice = choice.key;
      card.setAttribute('aria-label', `Choice: ${choice.text}`);
      card.textContent = choice.text;
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!activePromptKey) return;

        const promptKey = activePromptKey;
        const choiceKey = card.dataset.choice;
        const previousPromptForChoice = Object.keys(currentMatches).find((key) => currentMatches[key] === choiceKey);

        if (previousPromptForChoice && previousPromptForChoice !== promptKey) {
          delete currentMatches[previousPromptForChoice];
        }

        if (currentMatches[promptKey] === choiceKey) {
          delete currentMatches[promptKey];
          activePromptKey = null;
        } else {
          currentMatches[promptKey] = choiceKey;
          activePromptKey = null;
        }

        updateMatchStyles();
      });
      choiceCards[choice.key] = card;
      choicesCol.appendChild(card);
    });

    board.append(promptsCol, choicesCol);
    responseArea.append(board);

    const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check matches');
    submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
      window.sgilaCurrentMatches = { ...currentMatches };
      submitActivity(activity, { ...currentMatches });
    });
    responseArea.append(submit);
    return;
  }

  const board = document.createElement('div');
  board.className = 'matching-board';
  const words = document.createElement('div');
  words.className = 'matching-word-bank';
  const meanings = document.createElement('div');
  meanings.className = 'matching-meaning-bank';
  const assignments = {};
  const promptByChoice = {};
  const promptColors = ['blue', 'orange', 'yellow', 'purple'];
  const colorByPrompt = {};
  let selectedPrompt = '';
  const matchedPairs = document.createElement('div');
  matchedPairs.className = 'matching-pairs';

  const promptText = (promptKey) => (
    (config.prompts || []).find((prompt) => prompt.key === promptKey)?.text || promptKey
  );

  const updateBoard = () => {
    words.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((word) => {
      const promptKey = word.dataset.prompt;
      const choiceKey = assignments[promptKey] || '';
      word.dataset.choice = choiceKey;
      word.classList.toggle('is-selected', word.dataset.prompt === selectedPrompt);
      word.classList.toggle('is-matched', Boolean(choiceKey));
      word.hidden = Boolean(choiceKey);
    });
    meanings.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((meaning) => {
      const promptKey = promptByChoice[meaning.dataset.choice] || '';
      meaning.classList.toggle('is-matched', Boolean(promptKey));
      meaning.classList.toggle('is-selected', meaning.dataset.choice === assignments[selectedPrompt]);
      meaning.hidden = Boolean(promptKey);
      meaning.style.borderColor = promptKey ? colorByPrompt[promptKey] : '';
      meaning.style.backgroundColor = promptKey
        ? `color-mix(in srgb, ${colorByPrompt[promptKey]} 12%, var(--surface))`
        : '';
      meaning.setAttribute('aria-label', promptKey
        ? `${meaning.textContent}, matched with ${promptText(promptKey)}`
        : meaning.textContent);
    });

    matchedPairs.replaceChildren();
    Object.entries(assignments).forEach(([promptKey, choiceKey]) => {
      const color = colorByPrompt[promptKey];
      const pair = document.createElement('div');
      pair.className = 'matching-pair';
      pair.dataset.prompt = promptKey;
      pair.dataset.choice = choiceKey;

      const word = document.createElement('button');
      word.type = 'button';
      word.className = 'matching-pair-card matching-pair-word';
      word.style.borderColor = color;
      word.style.backgroundColor = `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 12%, var(--surface))`;
      word.setAttribute('aria-label', `Matched word ${promptText(promptKey)}. Select to change this match.`);
      const dot = document.createElement('span');
      dot.className = 'matching-dot';
      dot.style.backgroundColor = color;
      const wordLabel = document.createElement('span');
      wordLabel.textContent = promptText(promptKey);
      word.append(dot, wordLabel);

      const meaning = document.createElement('button');
      meaning.type = 'button';
      meaning.className = 'matching-pair-card';
      meaning.style.borderColor = color;
      meaning.style.backgroundColor = `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 12%, var(--surface))`;
      const choiceText = (config.choices || []).find((item) => item.key === choiceKey)?.text || choiceKey;
      meaning.setAttribute('aria-label', `Matched meaning ${choiceText}. Select to change this match.`);
      meaning.textContent = choiceText;

      const clearMatch = () => {
        delete assignments[promptKey];
        delete promptByChoice[choiceKey];
        selectedPrompt = promptKey;
        updateBoard();
        submit.disabled = Object.keys(assignments).length !== (config.prompts || []).length;
      };
      word.addEventListener('click', clearMatch);
      meaning.addEventListener('click', clearMatch);
      pair.append(word, meaning);
      matchedPairs.appendChild(pair);
    });
  };

  const assignChoice = (promptKey, choiceKey) => {
    if (!promptKey || !choiceKey) return;
    const previousChoice = assignments[promptKey];
    if (previousChoice) delete promptByChoice[previousChoice];
    const previousPrompt = promptByChoice[choiceKey];
    if (previousPrompt) delete assignments[previousPrompt];
    assignments[promptKey] = choiceKey;
    promptByChoice[choiceKey] = promptKey;
    selectedPrompt = promptKey;
    updateBoard();
    submit.disabled = Object.keys(assignments).length !== (config.prompts || []).length;
  };

  (config.prompts || []).forEach((prompt) => {
    const color = promptColors[Object.keys(colorByPrompt).length % promptColors.length];
    colorByPrompt[prompt.key] = color;
    const word = document.createElement('button');
    word.type = 'button';
    word.className = 'matching-word';
    word.dataset.prompt = prompt.key;
    word.draggable = true;
    word.style.borderColor = color;
    word.setAttribute('aria-label', `Word: ${prompt.text}`);
    const dot = document.createElement('span');
    dot.className = 'matching-dot';
    dot.style.backgroundColor = color;
    const label = document.createElement('span');
    label.textContent = prompt.text;
    word.append(dot, label);
    word.addEventListener('click', () => {
      selectedPrompt = prompt.key;
      updateBoard();
    });
    word.addEventListener('dragstart', (event) => {
      selectedPrompt = prompt.key;
      event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', prompt.key);
      updateBoard();
    });
    words.appendChild(word);
  });

  (config.choices || []).forEach((choice) => {
    const meaning = document.createElement('button');
    meaning.type = 'button';
    meaning.className = 'matching-meaning';
    meaning.dataset.choice = choice.key;
    meaning.textContent = choice.text;
    meaning.addEventListener('click', () => assignChoice(selectedPrompt, choice.key));
    meaning.addEventListener('pointerup', () => assignChoice(selectedPrompt, choice.key));
    meaning.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => event.preventDefault());
    meaning.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      assignChoice(event.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'), choice.key);
    });
    meanings.appendChild(meaning);
  });

  const wordsTitle = document.createElement('strong');
  wordsTitle.className = 'matching-column-title';
  wordsTitle.textContent = 'Words';
  words.prepend(wordsTitle);
  const meaningsTitle = document.createElement('strong');
  meaningsTitle.className = 'matching-column-title';
  meaningsTitle.textContent = 'Meanings';
  meanings.prepend(meaningsTitle);
  board.append(words, meanings);
  responseArea.append(board, matchedPairs);

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check matches');
  submit.disabled = true;
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    submitActivity(activity, assignments);
  });
  responseArea.append(submit);
}

function renderWordScramble(activity) {
  const config = activity.options || {};
  const rows = document.createElement('div');
  rows.className = 'scramble-rows';
  (config.items || []).forEach((item) => {
    const row = document.createElement('label');
    row.className = 'scramble-row';
    const letters = document.createElement('strong');
    letters.textContent = item.scramble.split('').join(' ');
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'text';
    input.autocomplete = 'off';
    input.dataset.item = item.key;
    input.placeholder = 'Type the word';
    row.append(letters, input);
    rows.appendChild(row);
  });
  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Check spelling');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const answer = {};
    rows.querySelectorAll('input').forEach((input) => answer[input.dataset.item] = input.value);
    submitActivity(activity, answer);
  });
  responseArea.append(rows, submit);
}

function renderOral(activity) {
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  const oral = document.createElement('div');
  oral.className = 'oral-response';
  const title = document.createElement('strong');
  title.textContent = config.focus_word ? `Say the word: ${config.focus_word}` : 'Say it in a full sentence';
  const help = document.createElement('span');
  help.textContent = config.focus_word
    ? 'Listen, say the word clearly, then tick it as complete.'
    : 'Tell the answer to a teacher, parent, or learning partner.';
  oral.append(title, help);

  const label = document.createElement('label');
  label.className = 'reading-response-label';
  label.setAttribute('for', 'oral-response');
  label.textContent = 'Your spoken answer';

  const answer = document.createElement('textarea');
  answer.id = 'oral-response';
  answer.className = 'reading-response';
  answer.rows = 3;
  answer.maxLength = 600;
  answer.placeholder = 'Speak or type your answer here.';

  const dictationWrap = attachDictationToTextarea(answer);

  const submit = makeSubmitButton(config.focus_word ? 'I said the word' : 'I said my answer');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const spokenText = (answer.value || '').trim();
    submitActivity(activity, spokenText || 'Answered aloud');
  });

  responseArea.append(label, dictationWrap || answer, submit);
}

function attachDictationToTextarea(textarea) {
  const SpeechRecognitionCtor = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition || window.mozSpeechRecognition;
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'dictation-wrap';

  const mic = document.createElement('button');
  mic.type = 'button';
  mic.className = 'btn btn-light dictation-btn';
  mic.textContent = 'ðŸŽ™ Speak';
  mic.title = 'Use microphone to dictate your answer';

  if (!SpeechRecognitionCtor) {
    mic.disabled = true;
    mic.textContent = 'Mic unavailable';
    mic.title = 'Speech recognition is not supported in this browser.';
    wrap.append(textarea, mic);
    return wrap;
  }

  let recognition = null;
  let listening = false;

  const stopListening = () => {
    if (recognition) {
      try { recognition.stop(); } catch (error) {}
    }
    listening = false;
    mic.textContent = 'ðŸŽ™ Speak again';
    mic.classList.remove('is-listening');
  };

  const startListening = () => {
    recognition = new SpeechRecognitionCtor();
    recognition.lang = 'en-ZA';
    recognition.continuous = false;
    recognition.interimResults = false;
    recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;

    recognition.onstart = () => {
      listening = true;
      mic.textContent = 'â¹ Stop';
      mic.classList.add('is-listening');
      textarea.focus();
    };

    recognition.onresult = (event) => {
      const transcript = Array.from(event.results)
        .map((result) => result[0].transcript)
        .join(' ')
        .trim();

      if (transcript) {
        textarea.value = transcript;
        textarea.focus();
      }
    };

    recognition.onerror = (event) => {
      const message = event.error === 'not-allowed'
        ? 'Microphone permission is needed to use speech-to-text.'
        : 'Speech recognition is not available right now. Please type your answer instead.';
      textarea.setAttribute('placeholder', message);
      stopListening();
      textarea.focus();
    };

    recognition.onend = () => {
      if (listening) stopListening();
    };

    try {
      recognition.start();
    } catch (error) {
      stopListening();
      textarea.focus();
    }
  };

  mic.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (listening) {
      stopListening();
      return;
    }
    startListening();
  });

  wrap.append(textarea, mic);
  return wrap;
}

function renderWritten(activity) {
  const label = document.createElement('label');
  label.className = 'reading-response-label';
  label.setAttribute('for', 'reading-response');
  label.textContent = activity.activity_type === 'prediction' ? 'Your prediction and clue' : 'Your answer';

  const answer = document.createElement('textarea');
  answer.id = 'reading-response';
  answer.className = 'reading-response';
  answer.rows = 3;
  answer.maxLength = 600;
  answer.placeholder = activity.activity_type === 'prediction'
    ? 'I think ... because ...'
    : (activity.activity_type === 'open_ended' ? 'Write two or more sentences. Use punctuation.' : 'Write one clear sentence.');

  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Compare my answer');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => submitActivity(activity, answer.value));
  responseArea.append(label, answer, submit);
}

function renderGuidedDiary(activity) {
  const config = activity.options || {};
  const diary = document.createElement('div');
  diary.className = 'guided-diary';

  const prompt = document.createElement('p');
  prompt.className = 'guided-diary-prompt';
  prompt.textContent = config.prompt || 'Write about a time you had a secret, like Mandu.';

  const greeting = document.createElement('strong');
  greeting.className = 'guided-diary-greeting';
  greeting.textContent = 'Dear Diary,';

  const feelingLine = document.createElement('label');
  feelingLine.className = 'diary-line diary-feeling-line';
  const feelingCopy = document.createElement('span');
  feelingCopy.textContent = 'Today I felt';
  const feeling = document.createElement('select');
  feeling.dataset.field = 'feeling';
  feeling.innerHTML = '<option value="">Choose a feeling</option>';
  (config.feelings || []).forEach((item) => {
    const option = document.createElement('option');
    option.value = item;
    option.textContent = item;
    feeling.appendChild(option);
  });
  const becauseCopy = document.createElement('span');
  becauseCopy.textContent = 'because';
  feelingLine.append(feelingCopy, feeling, becauseCopy);

  const because = document.createElement('textarea');
  because.className = 'diary-text-entry';
  because.dataset.field = 'because';
  because.rows = 2;
  because.maxLength = 300;
  because.placeholder = 'Explain what happened.';
  feelingLine.appendChild(because);

  const bestPart = document.createElement('label');
  bestPart.className = 'diary-line';
  bestPart.innerHTML = '<span>The best part of my day was</span>';
  const bestPartInput = document.createElement('textarea');
  bestPartInput.className = 'diary-text-entry';
  bestPartInput.dataset.field = 'best_part';
  bestPartInput.rows = 2;
  bestPartInput.maxLength = 300;
  bestPartInput.placeholder = 'Write the best part.';
  bestPart.appendChild(bestPartInput);

  const nextTime = document.createElement('label');
  nextTime.className = 'diary-line';
  nextTime.innerHTML = '<span>Next time I would like to</span>';
  const nextTimeInput = document.createElement('textarea');
  nextTimeInput.className = 'diary-text-entry';
  nextTimeInput.dataset.field = 'next_time';
  nextTimeInput.rows = 2;
  nextTimeInput.maxLength = 300;
  nextTimeInput.placeholder = 'Write what you would like to do.';
  nextTime.appendChild(nextTimeInput);

  const signature = document.createElement('label');
  signature.className = 'diary-line diary-signature';
  const signatureCopy = document.createElement('span');
  signatureCopy.textContent = 'From';
  const signatureInput = document.createElement('input');
  signatureInput.type = 'text';
  signatureInput.dataset.field = 'from_name';
  signatureInput.maxLength = 100;
  signatureInput.value = childName;
  signature.append(signatureCopy, signatureInput);

  diary.append(prompt, greeting, feelingLine, bestPart, nextTime, signature);
  const submit = makeSubmitButton('Save my diary');
  submit.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const answer = {};
    diary.querySelectorAll('[data-field]').forEach((field) => answer[field.dataset.field] = field.value);
    submitActivity(activity, answer);
  });
  responseArea.append(diary, submit);
}

function renderActivity() {
  if (current >= activities.length) {
    saveScore();
    updateProgress();
    const finalLink = document.querySelector('#results-area a');
    if (finalLink) finalLink.href = `/lessons/${lessonId}/results`;
    if (finalLink) finalLink.textContent = 'View my score';
    document.getElementById('question-area').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('results-copy').textContent = `You completed all ${activityGroups.length} story activities.`;
    setMascot('happy', 'You worked through the whole story.');
    return;
  }

  const activity = activities[current];
  const group = activityGroups.find((item) => item.indexes.includes(current));
  const totalInGroup = group ? group.indexes.length : 1;
  const questionNumber = group ? group.indexes.indexOf(current) + 1 : 1;
  responseArea.innerHTML = '';
  responseArea.dataset.activityType = activity.activity_type;
  messageArea.className = 'message-area hidden';
  messageArea.innerHTML = '';
  nextButton.classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('activity-number').textContent = `Activity ${group ? group.number : current + 1}`;
  document.getElementById('question-number').textContent = `Question ${questionNumber}/${totalInGroup}`;
  document.getElementById('skill-label').textContent = activityTitle(activity, group);
  const config = activityConfig(activity);
  const isGradeOnePhonics = config.presentation === 'grade1_phonics';
  const isMatchIt = activity.group_title === 'Match It!';
  const isFixMistake = activity.group_title === 'Fix the Mistake';
  const isVisualMatch = activity.group_title === 'Visual Matching';
  const instruction = isMatchIt
    ? 'Choose a match for all four, then check your answers. One option on the right belongs to no one. Match each one on the left to what happened on the right.'
    : isVisualMatch
      ? 'Look at the picture, then choose the word that names it.'
      : isFixMistake
        ? `One word in this sentence is wrong. Choose the word that belongs in the story. ${activity.question}`
        : config.instruction
          || typeInstructions[activity.activity_type]
          || 'Use the story to answer.';
  document.getElementById('activity-instruction').textContent = instruction;
  const questionText = document.getElementById('question-text');
  delete questionText.dataset.speechText;
  questionText.textContent = activity.question;
  questionText.classList.toggle('hidden', isGradeOnePhonics);
  setMascot('ready', 'Take your time and use the story.');
  updateProgress();
  renderActivityMedia(activity, questionText, instruction);

  if (activity.options && activity.options.writing_template) {
    renderGuidedDiary(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'crossword') {
    renderCrossword(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'matching' && !Array.isArray(activity.options)) {
    renderMatching(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'word_scramble') {
    renderWordScramble(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'sequencing') {
    renderSequence(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'cloze') {
    renderCloze(activity);
  } else if (activity.activity_type === 'oral_response') {
    renderOral(activity);
  } else if (['open_ended', 'prediction', 'reasoning'].includes(activity.activity_type)) {
    renderWritten(activity);
  } else {
    renderChoice(activity);
  }
}

function disableResponses() {
  responseArea.querySelectorAll('button, textarea, input, select').forEach((element) => element.disabled = true);
}

function markChoice(selectedButton, data) {
  if (!selectedButton) return;
  selectedButton.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
  if (!data.correct) {
    responseArea.querySelectorAll('.btn-option').forEach((button) => {
      if (button.dataset.answer === data.correct_answer) button.classList.add('choice-correct');
    });
  }
}

function markMatchingAnswers(data) {
  const expectedAnswers = data.correct_answer;
  if (!expectedAnswers || typeof expectedAnswers !== 'object') return;

  responseArea.querySelectorAll('.drag-match-target').forEach((target) => {
    const promptKey = target.dataset.prompt;
    if (!promptKey) return;
    const isCorrect = String(expectedAnswers[promptKey] || '') === target.dataset.choice;
    target.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');
    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
    badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
    target.appendChild(badge);
  });

  const gradeFourCards = responseArea.querySelectorAll('.matching-prompt-card');
  if (gradeFourCards.length) {
    gradeFourCards.forEach((card) => {
      const promptKey = card.dataset.prompt;
      const selection = window.sgilaCurrentMatches && window.sgilaCurrentMatches[promptKey];
      const choiceKey = selection || card.dataset.choice || '';
      const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[promptKey] || '');
      const isCorrect = choiceKey === correctValue;
      card.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');

      const badge = document.createElement('span');
      badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
      badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
      card.appendChild(badge);
    });
    return;
  }

  responseArea.querySelectorAll('.matching-pair[data-prompt]').forEach((pair) => {
    const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[pair.dataset.prompt] || '');
    const isCorrect = pair.dataset.choice === correctValue;
    pair.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');
    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
    badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
    pair.appendChild(badge);
  });

  responseArea.querySelectorAll('.matching-word:not([hidden])').forEach((word) => {
    const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[word.dataset.prompt] || '');
    const isCorrect = word.dataset.choice === correctValue;
    word.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');
    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
    badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
    word.appendChild(badge);
  });
  responseArea.querySelectorAll('.matching-row').forEach((row) => {
    if (row.dataset.choice !== undefined) {
      const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[row.dataset.prompt] || '');
      const isCorrect = row.dataset.choice === correctValue;
      row.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');
      const badge = document.createElement('span');
      badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
      badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
      row.querySelector('strong').appendChild(badge);
      return;
    }
    const select = row.querySelector('select');
    const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[select.dataset.prompt] || '');
    const isCorrect = select.value === correctValue;
    row.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'match-correct' : 'match-wrong');

    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'matching-result-badge';
    badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
    row.querySelector('strong').appendChild(badge);
  });
}

function normaliseCheckedWord(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[.,!?;:"'()[\]{}-]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

function markScrambleAnswers(data) {
  const expectedAnswers = data.correct_answer;
  if (
    responseArea.dataset.activityType !== 'word_scramble'
    || !expectedAnswers
    || typeof expectedAnswers !== 'object'
  ) return;

  responseArea.querySelectorAll('.scramble-row').forEach((row) => {
    const input = row.querySelector('input');
    const correctValue = String(expectedAnswers[input.dataset.item] || '');
    const isCorrect = normaliseCheckedWord(input.value) === normaliseCheckedWord(correctValue);
    row.classList.add(isCorrect ? 'scramble-correct' : 'scramble-wrong');

    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'scramble-result-badge';
    badge.textContent = isCorrect ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect';
    row.querySelector('strong').appendChild(badge);

    if (!isCorrect) {
      const correction = document.createElement('span');
      correction.className = 'scramble-correction';
      correction.textContent = `Correct spelling: ${correctValue}`;
      row.appendChild(correction);
    }
  });
}

function showFeedback(data) {
  messageArea.classList.remove('hidden', 'correct', 'wrong', 'review');
  messageArea.classList.add(data.review_required ? 'review' : (data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'));

  const heading = document.createElement('strong');
  heading.textContent = data.saved
    ? 'Diary saved'
    : data.review_required
    ? 'Compare your thinking'
    : (data.correct ? 'Correct' : (data.partial ? `${data.score_awarded} of ${data.score_possible} correct` : 'Look at the story answer'));
  const message = document.createElement('span');
  message.textContent = data.message;
  messageArea.append(heading, message);

  if (data.model_answer) {
    const model = document.createElement('p');
    model.className = 'model-answer';
    model.textContent = `${data.review_required ? 'Example answer' : 'Story answer'}: ${data.model_answer}`;
    messageArea.appendChild(model);
  }
}

async function submitActivity(activity, answer, selectedButton = null) {
  if (typeof answer === 'string' && !answer.trim()) {
    messageArea.className = 'message-area wrong';
    messageArea.textContent = 'Add your answer before continuing.';
    return;
  }
  if (answer && typeof answer === 'object' && !Array.isArray(answer)) {
    const values = Object.values(answer);
    if (!values.length || values.some((value) => !String(value || '').trim())) {
      messageArea.className = 'message-area wrong';
      messageArea.textContent = 'Complete every answer before checking this activity.';
      return;
    }
  }

  const isLegacy = !activity.activity_id;
  const endpoint = isLegacy ? '/api/check-answer' : '/api/check-reading-activity';
  const payload = {
    child_id: childId,
    lesson_id: lessonId,
    child_answer: answer,
  };
  if (isLegacy) payload.question_id = activity.question_id;
  else payload.activity_id = activity.activity_id;

  const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });
  const data = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) {
    messageArea.className = 'message-area wrong';
    messageArea.textContent = data.error || 'Your answer could not be checked.';
    return;
  }

  disableResponses();
  completedCount++;
  score = Number(data.comprehension_score || 0);
  markChoice(selectedButton, data);
  markMatchingAnswers(data);
  markScrambleAnswers(data);
  showFeedback(data);
  setMascot(data.review_required || data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.review_required ? 'Good thinking. Compare your answer.' : (data.correct ? 'Great reading!' : 'Use the story answer to learn.'));
  const nextActivity = activities[current + 1];
  nextButton.textContent = current === activities.length - 1
    ? 'Finish activities'
    : (nextActivity.group_number === activity.group_number ? 'Next question' : `Start Activity ${nextActivity.group_number}`);
  nextButton.classList.remove('hidden');
  updateProgress();
}

async function loadActivities() {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/questions`, {cache: 'no-store'});
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Activities could not be loaded.');
    activities = await response.json();
    const groups = new Map();
    activities.forEach((activity, index) => {
      activity.group_number = Number(activity.group_number || index + 1);
      activity.group_title = activity.group_title || 'Story activity';
      if (!groups.has(activity.group_number)) {
        groups.set(activity.group_number, {number: activity.group_number, title: activity.group_title, indexes: []});
      }
      groups.get(activity.group_number).indexes.push(index);
    });
    activityGroups = [...groups.values()];
    if (!activities.length) {
      document.getElementById('question-area').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('results-copy').textContent = 'No comprehension activities have been added yet.';
      updateProgress();
      return;
    }
    renderActivity();
  } catch (error) {
    messageArea.className = 'message-area wrong';
    messageArea.textContent = error.message;
  }
}

nextButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (current >= activities.length - 1) {
    window.location.assign(`/lessons/${lessonId}/results`);
    return;
  }
  current++;
  renderActivity();
});

window.onerror = function(msg, url, line, col, error) {
  console.error('Global error:', msg, error);
  if (messageArea) {
    messageArea.className = 'message-area wrong';
    messageArea.textContent = 'Error: ' + msg;
  }
};

loadActivities();
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<section class="auth-layout role-{{ role }}">
  <aside class="auth-intro">
    <img class="auth-koaly-peek" src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt="" aria-hidden="true">
    <p class="eyebrow">Create account</p>
    <h1>Join SGILA</h1>
    <p>Choose the account type that matches your role.</p>

      <a class="{% if role == 'parent' %}active{% endif %}" href="/register?role=parent">Parent</a>
      <a class="{% if role == 'teacher' %}active{% endif %}" href="/register?role=teacher">Teacher</a>
    </div>
  </aside>

  <form class="form-card auth-card-blue" method="POST" action="/register">
    {% csrf_token %}
    <input type="hidden" name="role" value="{{ role }}">

{% if role == 'parent' %}
      <h2>Parent registration</h2>
      <label>Full name<input type="text" name="full_name" required></label>
      <label>Email<input type="email" name="email" required></label>
      <label>Phone<input type="text" name="phone"></label>
      <label>Password
        <div class="password-field-wrap">
          <input type="password" name="password" id="password" required minlength="8"
                 autocomplete="new-password"
                 aria-describedby="password-strength password-requirements">
          <button type="button" class="password-toggle-visibility" data-target="password" aria-label="Show password">Show</button>
        </div>
      </label>
      <div class="password-actions">
        <button type="button" class="generate-password-btn" data-target="password">Suggest a strong password</button>

      </div>
      <div id="password-strength" class="password-strength" aria-live="polite">
        <div class="strength-bar"><div class="strength-bar-fill" id="strength-bar-fill"></div></div>
        <span id="strength-label" class="strength-label"></span>
      </div>
      <ul id="password-requirements" class="password-requirements">

      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-full" type="submit" id="submit-btn-parent" disabled>Create parent account</button>
    {% else %}
      <h2>Teacher registration</h2>

      <label>Work email<input type="email" name="email" required></label>
      <div class="teacher-school-picker" id="teacher-school-picker">
        <label for="teacher-school-search">School name</label>
        <div class="teacher-school-control">
          <input id="teacher-school-search" type="text" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search or enter your school" role="combobox" aria-autocomplete="list" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="teacher-school-results" required>
          <input id="teacher-school-name" type="hidden" name="school_name">
          <div id="teacher-school-results" class="teacher-school-results" role="listbox" aria-label="School search results" hidden></div>
        </div>
        <p id="teacher-school-status" class="teacher-school-status" role="status" aria-live="polite">Search for your school, or type its name if it is not listed.</p>
        <p id="teacher-school-quantile" class="teacher-school-quantile" aria-live="polite" hidden></p>
      </div>

      <label>Password
        <div class="password-field-wrap">
          <input type="password" name="password" id="password" required minlength="8"
                 autocomplete="new-password"
                 aria-describedby="password-strength password-requirements">
          <button type="button" class="password-toggle-visibility" data-target="password" aria-label="Show password">Show</button>
        </div>
      </label>
      <div class="password-actions">
        <button type="button" class="generate-password-btn" data-target="password">Suggest a strong password</button>
        <button type="button" class="copy-password-btn" data-target="password">Copy to clipboard</button>
      </div>
      <div id="password-strength" class="password-strength" aria-live="polite">
        <div class="strength-bar"><div class="strength-bar-fill" id="strength-bar-fill"></div></div>
        <span id="strength-label" class="strength-label"></span>
      </div>
      <ul id="password-requirements" class="password-requirements">
        <li data-rule="length">At least 8 characters</li>
        <li data-rule="uppercase">One uppercase letter</li>
        <li data-rule="lowercase">One lowercase letter</li>
        <li data-rule="number">One number</li>
        <li data-rule="special">One special character (!@#$%^&* etc.)</li>
      </ul>
      <p id="password-suggestion" class="password-suggestion" hidden></p>

      <label class="terms-checkbox-label">
        <input type="checkbox" name="accepted_popia" id="accepted_popia" value="true" required>
        I accept SGILA's <a href="/terms" class="terms-link" target="_blank">terms and conditions</a>.
      </label>

      <button class="btn btn-mint btn-full" type="submit" id="submit-btn-teacher" disabled>Create teacher account</button>
    {% endif %}
    <p class="form-note">Already have an account? <a href="/login?role={{ role }}">Sign in</a></p>
  </form>
</section>

<script>
(function () {
  const schoolSearch = document.getElementById('teacher-school-search');
  if (schoolSearch) {
    const schoolPicker = document.getElementById('teacher-school-picker');
    const schoolName = document.getElementById('teacher-school-name');
    const schoolResults = document.getElementById('teacher-school-results');
    const schoolStatus = document.getElementById('teacher-school-status');
    const schoolQuantile = document.getElementById('teacher-school-quantile');
    const registrationForm = schoolSearch.closest('form');
    let searchTimer;
    let searchSequence = 0;
    let activeSchoolIndex = -1;

    function closeSchoolResults() {
      schoolResults.hidden = true;
      schoolSearch.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      schoolSearch.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
      activeSchoolIndex = -1;
    }

    function setActiveSchoolOption(index) {
      const options = [...schoolResults.querySelectorAll('.teacher-school-option')];
      if (!options.length) return;
      activeSchoolIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, options.length - 1));
      options.forEach((option, optionIndex) => {
        const isActive = optionIndex === activeSchoolIndex;
        option.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
        option.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(isActive));
      });
      schoolSearch.setAttribute('aria-activedescendant', options[activeSchoolIndex].id);
    }

    function selectSchool(school) {
      schoolSearch.value = school.school_name;
      schoolName.value = school.school_name;
      schoolStatus.textContent = school.location
        ? `Selected from the directory: ${school.location}.`
        : 'Selected from the national school directory.';
      schoolQuantile.textContent = school.quantile
        ? `School quantile: ${school.quantile} (2025 National School Directory).`
        : 'This school has no quantile listed in the 2025 National School Directory.';
      schoolQuantile.hidden = false;
      closeSchoolResults();
      schoolSearch.focus();
    }

    function renderSchoolResults(schools, query) {
      schoolResults.replaceChildren();
      if (!schools.length) {
        schoolStatus.textContent = `No listed school matches "${query}". You can still register with this school name; its quantile will be unavailable.`;
        closeSchoolResults();
        return;
      }

      schools.forEach((school, index) => {
        const option = document.createElement('div');
        option.id = `teacher-school-option-${index}`;
        option.className = 'teacher-school-option';
        option.setAttribute('role', 'option');
        option.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');
        option.tabIndex = -1;

        const name = document.createElement('span');
        name.className = 'teacher-school-option-name';
        name.textContent = school.school_name;
        const detail = document.createElement('span');
        detail.className = 'teacher-school-option-detail';
        detail.textContent = [school.location, school.quantile || 'Quantile unavailable'].filter(Boolean).join(' Â· ');
        option.append(name, detail);
        option.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => event.preventDefault());
        option.addEventListener('click', () => selectSchool(school));
        schoolResults.append(option);
      });

      schoolStatus.textContent = 'Select your school to see its quantile, or keep typing to enter an unlisted school.';
      schoolSearch.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
      schoolResults.hidden = false;
    }

    schoolSearch.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const query = schoolSearch.value.trim();
      schoolName.value = query;
      schoolQuantile.hidden = true;
      schoolQuantile.textContent = '';
      closeSchoolResults();
      const sequence = ++searchSequence;
      window.clearTimeout(searchTimer);

      if (query.length < 2) {
        schoolStatus.textContent = query
          ? 'Enter at least two characters to search, or continue with a custom school name.'
          : 'Search for your school, or type its name if it is not listed.';
        return;
      }

      schoolStatus.textContent = 'Searching the national school directory...';
      searchTimer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(`/api/schools/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`, {
            headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
          });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('School search is unavailable.');
          const payload = await response.json();
          if (sequence !== searchSequence || schoolSearch.value.trim() !== query) return;
          renderSchoolResults(payload.results || [], query);
        } catch (error) {
          if (sequence !== searchSequence) return;
          schoolStatus.textContent = 'School search is unavailable. You can still enter your school name; its quantile will be unavailable.';
        }
      }, 180);
    });

    schoolSearch.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      const options = schoolResults.querySelectorAll('.teacher-school-option');
      if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' && !schoolResults.hidden && options.length) {
        event.preventDefault();
        setActiveSchoolOption(activeSchoolIndex + 1);
      } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp' && !schoolResults.hidden && options.length) {
        event.preventDefault();
        setActiveSchoolOption(activeSchoolIndex < 0 ? options.length - 1 : activeSchoolIndex - 1);
      } else if (event.key === 'Enter' && !schoolResults.hidden && activeSchoolIndex >= 0) {
        event.preventDefault();
        options[activeSchoolIndex].click();
      } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        closeSchoolResults();
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      if (!schoolPicker.contains(event.target)) closeSchoolResults();
    });

    registrationForm.addEventListener('submit', () => {
      if (!schoolName.value.trim()) schoolName.value = schoolSearch.value.trim();
    });
  }

  const SPECIAL_RE = /[!@#$%^&*()_+\-=\[\]{};':"\\|,.<>\/?~`]/;

  function evaluatePassword(pw) {
    const rules = {
      length: pw.length >= 8,
      uppercase: /[A-Z]/.test(pw),
      lowercase: /[a-z]/.test(pw),
      number: /[0-9]/.test(pw),
      special: SPECIAL_RE.test(pw)
    };
    const metCount = Object.values(rules).filter(Boolean).length;
    const allMet = metCount === 5;

    let score = 0;
    if (rules.length) score++;
    if (pw.length >= 12) score++;
    if (rules.uppercase) score++;
    if (rules.lowercase) score++;
    if (rules.number) score++;
    if (rules.special) score++;

    let label, strength;
    if (pw.length === 0) {
      label = ''; strength = 'empty';
    } else if (!allMet) {
      label = 'Too weak'; strength = 'weak';
    } else if (score <= 5) {
      label = 'Okay'; strength = 'okay';
    } else if (score === 6) {
      label = 'Strong'; strength = 'strong';
    } else {
      label = 'Very strong'; strength = 'very-strong';
    }

    return { rules, allMet, label, strength };
  }

  function suggestionFor(pw, rules) {
    const missing = [];
    if (!rules.length) missing.push('at least 8 characters');
    if (!rules.uppercase) missing.push('an uppercase letter');
    if (!rules.lowercase) missing.push('a lowercase letter');
    if (!rules.number) missing.push('a number');
    if (!rules.special) missing.push('a special character');
    if (missing.length === 0) {
      return pw.length < 12 ? 'Tip: use 12+ characters for a stronger password.' : '';
    }
    return 'Add ' + missing.join(', ') + ' to strengthen your password.';
  }

  const UPPER = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ';           // no I/O to avoid look-alikes
  const LOWER = 'abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz';           // no l
  const DIGITS = '23456789';                           // no 0/1
  const SPECIAL = '!@#$%^&*()-_=+?';
  const ALL_CHARS = UPPER + LOWER + DIGITS + SPECIAL;

  function secureRandomInt(max) {
    const arr = new Uint32Array(1);
    crypto.getRandomValues(arr);
    return arr[0] % max;
  }

  function pickChar(charset) {
    return charset[secureRandomInt(charset.length)];
  }

  function shuffle(chars) {
    const arr = chars.slice();
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = secureRandomInt(i + 1);
      [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
    }
    return arr;
  }

  function generateStrongPassword(length) {
    length = length || 14;
    // Guarantee at least one of each required character class first
    const required = [
      pickChar(UPPER),
      pickChar(LOWER),
      pickChar(DIGITS),
      pickChar(SPECIAL)
    ];
    const remaining = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < length - required.length; i++) {
      remaining.push(pickChar(ALL_CHARS));
    }
    return shuffle(required.concat(remaining)).join('');
  }

  function wireForm(form) {
    const pwInput = form.querySelector('#password');
    const barFill = form.querySelector('#strength-bar-fill');
    const label = form.querySelector('#strength-label');
    const suggestion = form.querySelector('#password-suggestion');
    const reqItems = form.querySelectorAll('.password-requirements li');
    const checkbox = form.querySelector('#accepted_popia');
    const submitBtn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    const generateBtn = form.querySelector('.generate-password-btn');
    const toggleBtn = form.querySelector('.password-toggle-visibility');
    const copyBtn = form.querySelector('.copy-password-btn');

    if (!pwInput || !barFill || !label || !checkbox || !submitBtn) {
      console.warn('[password-strength] Required elements missing in form â€” skipping wiring.', form);
      return;
    }

    function updateSubmitState() {
      const result = evaluatePassword(pwInput.value);
      submitBtn.disabled = !(result.allMet && checkbox.checked);
    }

    pwInput.addEventListener('input', function () {
      const pw = pwInput.value;
      const result = evaluatePassword(pw);

      barFill.dataset.strength = result.strength;
      label.textContent = result.label;
      label.dataset.strength = result.strength;

      reqItems.forEach(li => {
        const rule = li.getAttribute('data-rule');
        li.classList.toggle('met', !!result.rules[rule]);
      });

      const tip = suggestionFor(pw, result.rules);
      if (tip) {
        suggestion.textContent = tip;
        suggestion.hidden = false;
      } else {
        suggestion.hidden = true;
      }

      pwInput.setCustomValidity(result.allMet ? '' : 'Password does not meet the minimum strength requirements.');
      updateSubmitState();
    });

    if (generateBtn) {
      generateBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        const newPassword = generateStrongPassword(14);
        pwInput.type = 'text';
        pwInput.value = newPassword;
        if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.textContent = 'Hide';
        // Trigger the same input handler so the strength meter/checklist update
        pwInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
        pwInput.focus();
      });
    }

    if (toggleBtn) {
      toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        const isHidden = pwInput.type === 'password';
        pwInput.type = isHidden ? 'text' : 'password';
        toggleBtn.textContent = isHidden ? 'Hide' : 'Show';
      });
    }

    if (copyBtn) {
      copyBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        if (!pwInput.value) return;

        function showCopiedFeedback() {
          const original = copyBtn.textContent;
          copyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
          copyBtn.classList.add('copied');
          setTimeout(function () {
            copyBtn.textContent = original;
            copyBtn.classList.remove('copied');
          }, 1500);
        }

        if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(pwInput.value).then(showCopiedFeedback).catch(function () {
            fallbackCopy();
          });
        } else {
          fallbackCopy();
        }

        function fallbackCopy() {
          const wasType = pwInput.type;
          pwInput.type = 'text';
          pwInput.select();
          pwInput.setSelectionRange(0, pwInput.value.length);
          try {
            document.execCommand('copy');
            showCopiedFeedback();
          } catch (err) {
            // Copy failed silently â€” user can still select and copy manually
          }
          pwInput.type = wasType;
        }
      });
    }

    checkbox.addEventListener('change', updateSubmitState);

    form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
      const result = evaluatePassword(pwInput.value);
      if (!result.allMet || !checkbox.checked) {
        e.preventDefault();
        pwInput.reportValidity();
      }
    });
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('#password').forEach(function (pwInput) {
    const form = pwInput.closest('form');
    if (!form) {
      console.warn('[password-strength] #password input is not inside a <form> element â€” skipping.', pwInput);
      return;
    }
    wireForm(form);
  });
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
  const np = document.getElementById('new_password');
  const cp = document.getElementById('confirm_password');
  const hint = document.getElementById('mismatch-hint');
  const btn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');

  function check() {
    const match = np.value === cp.value;
    hint.classList.toggle('hidden', cp.value.length === 0 || match);
    btn.disabled = !match || np.value.length < 6;
  }
  np.addEventListener('input', check);
  cp.addEventListener('input', check);
  check();
</script>

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\sequencing.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Sequencing - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell sequencing-page">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Sequencing</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Put the events in the correct order</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <p class="instruction-text">Drag the events into the correct order, then press Check.</p>
  <ul id="event-list" class="sequence-list"></ul>
  <div class="center activity-action-row">
    <button id="check-btn" class="btn btn-primary" type="button">Check my order</button>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden activity-action-row">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/inference" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
