<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let activityId = null;
let dragSrc = null;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/sequencing`);
  const data = await res.json();
  activityId = data.id;
  const list = document.getElementById('event-list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  data.events.forEach(evt => {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.textContent = evt;
    li.draggable = true;
    li.addEventListener('dragstart', e => { dragSrc = li; li.style.opacity = '0.5'; });
    li.addEventListener('dragend', () => li.style.opacity = '1');
    li.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); li.classList.add('drag-over'); });
    li.addEventListener('dragleave', () => li.classList.remove('drag-over'));
    li.addEventListener('drop', e => {
      e.preventDefault();
      li.classList.remove('drag-over');
      if (dragSrc !== li) list.insertBefore(dragSrc, li);
    });
    list.appendChild(li);
  });
}

document.getElementById('check-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('#event-list li')].map(li => li.textContent);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-sequencing-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({activity_id: activityId, submitted_order: items}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Perfect!' : 'Check below');
  document.querySelectorAll('#event-list li').forEach((li, i) => {
    li.classList.add(li.textContent === data.correct_order[i] ? 'seq-correct' : 'seq-wrong');
    li.draggable = false;
  });
  document.getElementById('check-btn').disabled = true;
  document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
});

load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Spelling - SGILA{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/pronunciation" class="back-link">Back</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Spelling</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Complete the spelling activities</h1>
  <div id="spelling-koaly" class="reactive-koaly koaly-ready">
    <img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt="Koaly mascot">
    <div>
      <strong>Koaly is ready</strong>
      <span>Type your answer, then check it before finishing.</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <form id="spelling-form" method="POST" class="spelling-form">
    {% csrf_token %}
    {% for activity in activities %}
      <label
        class="spelling-card"
        data-activity-id="{{ activity.id }}"
        data-activity-type="{{ activity.activity_type }}"
      >
        <span>{{ activity.get_activity_type_display }}</span>
        <strong>{{ activity.display_text|default:activity.answer }}</strong>
        <input type="text" name="activity_{{ activity.id }}" placeholder="Type your answer" autocomplete="off" required>
        <div class="spelling-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-light check-spelling" type="button">Check answer</button>
          <p class="spelling-feedback message-area hidden" aria-live="polite"></p>
        </div>
      </label>
    {% empty %}
      <p class="empty">No spelling activities yet.</p>
    {% endfor %}
    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-full" type="submit">Finish lesson</button>
  </form>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const childId = {{ child.id }};
const storageKey = `sgila_lesson_${lessonId}`;
const spellingKoaly = document.getElementById('spelling-koaly');
const KOALY_IMAGES = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad: "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let answerAudioContext = null;

function setSpellingKoaly(state, title, detail) {
  spellingKoaly.className = `reactive-koaly koaly-${state}`;
  spellingKoaly.querySelector('img').src = KOALY_IMAGES[state] || KOALY_IMAGES.ready;
  spellingKoaly.querySelector('strong').textContent = title;
  spellingKoaly.querySelector('span').textContent = detail;
}

function primeAnswerAudio() {
  const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
  if (!AudioContext) return null;
  if (!answerAudioContext) answerAudioContext = new AudioContext();
  if (answerAudioContext.state === 'suspended') answerAudioContext.resume();
  return answerAudioContext;
}

function playTone(ctx, start, frequency, duration, type, volume) {
  const oscillator = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  oscillator.type = type;
  oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, start);
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume, start + 0.025);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + duration);
  oscillator.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  oscillator.start(start);
  oscillator.stop(start + duration + 0.02);
}

function playAnswerSound(correct) {
  const ctx = primeAnswerAudio();
  if (!ctx) return;
  const now = ctx.currentTime + 0.02;
  if (correct) {
    playTone(ctx, now, 523.25, 0.12, 'sine', 0.12);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.1, 659.25, 0.13, 'sine', 0.12);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.21, 783.99, 0.18, 'triangle', 0.10);
  } else {
    playTone(ctx, now, 220, 0.16, 'triangle', 0.10);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.15, 164.81, 0.22, 'sine', 0.08);
  }
}

async function checkSpellingCard(card) {
  primeAnswerAudio();
  const input = card.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
  const feedback = card.querySelector('.spelling-feedback');
  const checkButton = card.querySelector('.check-spelling');
  const given = input.value.trim();

  card.classList.remove('is-correct', 'is-wrong');
  input.classList.remove('input-correct', 'input-wrong');

  if (!given) {
    feedback.className = 'spelling-feedback message-area wrong';
    feedback.textContent = 'Type your answer first.';
    setSpellingKoaly('sad', 'Try typing first', 'Koaly is waiting for your answer.');
    return;
  }

  checkButton.disabled = true;
  let data;
  try {
    const response = await fetch('/api/check-spelling-answer', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({
        child_id: childId,
        lesson_id: lessonId,
        activity_id: card.dataset.activityId,
        answer: input.value,
      }),
    });
    data = await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || data.error || 'Unable to check this answer.');
  } catch (error) {
    feedback.className = 'spelling-feedback message-area wrong';
    feedback.textContent = error.message;
    setSpellingKoaly('sad', 'Try again', 'Koaly could not check that answer yet.');
    checkButton.disabled = false;
    return;
  }

  const correct = data.correct;
  card.classList.add(correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong');
  input.classList.add(correct ? 'input-correct' : 'input-wrong');
  feedback.className = `spelling-feedback message-area ${correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  feedback.textContent = data.message;
  setSpellingKoaly(
    correct ? 'happy' : 'sad',
    correct ? 'Great spelling!' : 'Try again',
    correct ? 'Koaly is proud of that answer.' : 'Koaly thinks you should look carefully and try once more.'
  );
  playAnswerSound(correct);
  checkButton.disabled = false;
}

document.querySelectorAll('.spelling-card').forEach((card) => {
  const input = card.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
  const checkButton = card.querySelector('.check-spelling');
  checkButton.addEventListener('click', () => checkSpellingCard(card));
  input.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
      event.preventDefault();
      checkSpellingCard(card);
    }
  });
  input.addEventListener('input', () => {
    card.classList.remove('is-correct', 'is-wrong');
    input.classList.remove('input-correct', 'input-wrong');
    card.querySelector('.spelling-feedback').className = 'spelling-feedback message-area hidden';
    setSpellingKoaly('ready', 'Koaly is ready', 'Type your answer, then check it before finishing.');
  });
});

document.getElementById('spelling-form').addEventListener('submit', () => {
  sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}{{ lesson.title }} - SGILA{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/grade/{{ lesson.grade }}" class="back-link">Back to lessons</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Story</span>
  </div>
  <h1>{{ lesson.title }}</h1>

  <div id="story-koaly" class="reactive-koaly koaly-ready">
    <img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt="Koaly mascot">
    <div>
      <strong>Koaly is ready</strong>
      <span>Press play to hear the playful story voice and follow the highlighted words.</span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div id="page-display" class="story-book">
    {% for page in pages %}
      <article class="story-page {% if not forloop.first %}hidden{% endif %}" data-audio="{{ page.audio_url }}" data-page="{{ page.page_number }}">
        <div class="story-image{% if '/storyboards/' in page.image_url %} storyboard-crop{% if '#panel-2' in page.image_url %} storyboard-panel-2{% elif '#panel-3' in page.image_url %} storyboard-panel-3{% elif '#panel-4' in page.image_url %} storyboard-panel-4{% elif '#panel-5' in page.image_url %} storyboard-panel-5{% elif '#panel-6' in page.image_url %} storyboard-panel-6{% else %} storyboard-panel-1{% endif %}{% endif %}">
          {% if page.image_url %}
            <img src="{{ page.image_url }}" alt="Story illustration for page {{ page.page_number }}">
          {% else %}
            <span>Story image</span>
          {% endif %}
        </div>
        <p class="story-text" data-words="{{ page.highlighted_words|join:',' }}">{{ page.text }}</p>
        <div class="audio-bar">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-mint play-voiceover">Play story voice</button>
          <span>Highlighted words: {{ page.highlighted_words|join:", " }}</span>
        </div>
      </article>
    {% empty %}
      <article class="story-page">
        <div class="story-image"><span>Story image</span></div>
        <p class="story-text" data-words="">No story pages have been added yet.</p>
      </article>
    {% endfor %}
  </div>

  <div class="story-nav">
    <button id="prev-btn" class="btn btn-light" disabled>Previous</button>
    <span id="page-counter">{% if pages %}Page 1 of {{ pages|length }}{% else %}No pages yet{% endif %}</span>
    <button id="next-btn" class="btn btn-primary {% if not pages %}hidden{% endif %}">Next</button>
  </div>
  <div id="story-done" class="center {% if pages %}hidden{% endif %}">
    <a href="{{ activity_resume_url|default:first_activity_url }}" class="btn btn-primary btn-large">
      {% if lesson.grade == 1 %}Start 3 activities{% else %}Answer questions{% endif %}
    </a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const pages = document.querySelectorAll('.story-page');
let current = 0;
let activeUtterance = null;
let activeTimer = null;
const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prev-btn');
const nextBtn = document.getElementById('next-btn');
const counter = document.getElementById('page-counter');
const doneDiv = document.getElementById('story-done');
const koaly = document.getElementById('story-koaly');
const KOALY_IMAGES = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  listening: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad: "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};

let availableVoices = [];
function loadVoices() {
  availableVoices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
}
if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
  loadVoices();
  window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = loadVoices;
}

function cleanWord(word) {
  return word.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z']/g, '');
}

function setKoaly(state, title, detail) {
  koaly.className = `reactive-koaly koaly-${state}`;
  koaly.querySelector('img').src = KOALY_IMAGES[state] || KOALY_IMAGES.ready;
  koaly.querySelector('strong').textContent = title;
  koaly.querySelector('span').textContent = detail;
}

function stopVoiceover() {
  if (activeTimer) {
    clearInterval(activeTimer);
    activeTimer = null;
  }
  if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  }
  activeUtterance = null;
}

function getFemaleVoice(lang = 'en') {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return null;

  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const langPrefix = lang.split('-')[0].toLowerCase();

  const femaleKeywords = [
    'zira', 'jenny', 'aria', 'samantha', 'sonia', 'fiona',
    'victoria', 'karen', 'moira', 'tessa', 'serena', 'hazel',
    'susan', 'linda', 'amy', 'emma', 'female',
    'google uk english female', 'google us english female'
  ];

  const maleKeywords = ['david', 'mark', 'james', 'daniel', 'george',
    'richard', 'thomas', 'male', 'guy', 'fred', 'alex'];

  function isFemale(voice) {
    const name = voice.name.toLowerCase();
    const hasFemaleKeyword = femaleKeywords.some((kw) => name.includes(kw));
    const hasMaleKeyword = maleKeywords.some((kw) => name.includes(kw));
    return hasFemaleKeyword && !hasMaleKeyword;
  }

  const match1 = voices.find((v) =>
    v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(langPrefix) && isFemale(v)
  );
  if (match1) return match1;

  const match2 = voices.find((v) =>
    v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith('en') && isFemale(v)
  );
  if (match2) return match2;

  const match3 = voices.find((v) => isFemale(v));
  if (match3) return match3;

 
  return null;
}

function applyKidFriendlySpeechSettings(utterance, lang = 'en-ZA') {
  utterance.lang = lang;
  utterance.rate = 0.84;
  utterance.pitch = 1.35;
  utterance.volume = 1;
  const femaleVoice = getFemaleVoice(lang);
  if (femaleVoice) {
    utterance.voice = femaleVoice;
  }
}

function prepareStoryWords() {
  pages.forEach((page) => {
    const textEl = page.querySelector('.story-text');
    const rawText = textEl.textContent.trim();
    const focusWords = textEl.dataset.words.split(',').map((w) => cleanWord(w.trim())).filter(Boolean);
    textEl.dataset.rawText = rawText;
    textEl.innerHTML = '';
    rawText.split(/(\s+)/).forEach((token) => {
      if (/^\s+$/.test(token)) {
        textEl.appendChild(document.createTextNode(token));
        return;
      }
      const span = document.createElement('span');
      span.className = 'story-word';
      span.textContent = token;
      if (focusWords.includes(cleanWord(token))) {
        span.classList.add('focus-word');
      }
      textEl.appendChild(span);
    });
  });
}

function clearWordHighlights(page) {
  page.querySelectorAll('.story-word').forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('spoken-word', 'current-word'));
}

function highlightWord(page, index) {
  const words = [...page.querySelectorAll('.story-word')];
  words.forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('current-word'));
  if (!words[index]) return;
  words[index].classList.add('spoken-word', 'current-word');
}

function playPage(page) {
  stopVoiceover();
  clearWordHighlights(page);
  const textEl = page.querySelector('.story-text');
  const words = [...page.querySelectorAll('.story-word')];
  const rawText = textEl.dataset.rawText || textEl.textContent.trim();
  const playButton = page.querySelector('.play-voiceover');

  setKoaly('listening', 'Koaly is reading', 'Listen and follow each highlighted word.');
  playButton.textContent = 'Reading...';

  activeUtterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(rawText);
  applyKidFriendlySpeechSettings(activeUtterance);

  activeUtterance.onboundary = (event) => {
    if (event.name !== 'word') return;
    const charIndex = event.charIndex;
    let wordIndex = 0;
    let charCount = 0;
    rawText.split(/(\s+)/).forEach((token) => {
      if (/^\s+$/.test(token)) {
        charCount += token.length;
        return;
      }
      if (charCount <= charIndex && charIndex < charCount + token.length) {
        words.forEach((w) => w.classList.remove('current-word'));
        if (words[wordIndex]) {
          words[wordIndex].classList.add('spoken-word', 'current-word');
        }
      }
      charCount += token.length;
      wordIndex++;
    });
  };
  activeUtterance.onend = () => {
    words.forEach((word) => word.classList.remove('current-word'));
    setKoaly('happy', 'Great listening', 'Now try the next page when you are ready.');
    playButton.textContent = 'Replay story voice';
  };
  activeUtterance.onerror = () => {
    setKoaly('sad', 'Keep reading', 'Voiceover is not available, but the words can still guide you.');
    playButton.textContent = 'Replay story voice';
  };
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(activeUtterance);
}

function showPage(n) {
  if (!pages[n]) return;
  stopVoiceover();
  pages.forEach((p) => p.classList.add('hidden'));
  pages[n].classList.remove('hidden');
  clearWordHighlights(pages[n]);
  prevBtn.disabled = n === 0;
  counter.textContent = `Page ${n + 1} of ${pages.length}`;
  nextBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', n === pages.length - 1);
  doneDiv.classList.toggle('hidden', n !== pages.length - 1);
  pages[n].querySelector('.play-voiceover').textContent = 'Play story voice';
  setKoaly('ready', 'Koaly is ready', 'Press play to hear the playful story voice and follow the highlighted words.');
}

nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if (current < pages.length - 1) showPage(++current); });
prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if (current > 0) showPage(--current); });
document.querySelectorAll('.play-voiceover').forEach((button) => {
  button.addEventListener('click', () => playPage(button.closest('.story-page')));
});
prepareStoryWords();
</script>
{% endblock %}

                 placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456" autocomplete="cc-number">

                   placeholder="123" autocomplete="cc-csc">

          <input type="text" name="account_holder">

<script>
(function () {
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#payment-tabs a');
  const methodInput = document.getElementById('payment-method-input');
  const cardFields = document.getElementById('card-fields');
  const debitFields = document.getElementById('debit-fields');

  tabs.forEach((tab) => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      tabs.forEach((t) => t.classList.remove('active'));
      tab.classList.add('active');
      const method = tab.dataset.method;
      methodInput.value = method;
      cardFields.classList.toggle('is-hidden', method !== 'card');
      debitFields.classList.toggle('is-hidden', method !== 'debit_order');
    });
  });

  // Light formatting so the card number is easier to read as it's typed.
  const cardNumberInput = document.querySelector('input[name="card_number"]');
  if (cardNumberInput) {
    cardNumberInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const digits = cardNumberInput.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 19);
      cardNumberInput.value = digits.replace(/(.{4})/g, '$1 ').trim();
    });
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  const dialog = document.getElementById('deactivate-dialog');
  const openButton = document.getElementById('open-deactivate-dialog');
  const closeButton = document.getElementById('close-deactivate-dialog');
  if (dialog && openButton && closeButton) {
    openButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
      if (typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') dialog.showModal();
      else dialog.setAttribute('open', '');
    });
    closeButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
      if (typeof dialog.close === 'function') dialog.close();
      else dialog.removeAttribute('open');
    });
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const codeText = document.getElementById('teacher-code-value');
    const shareButton = document.getElementById('share-teacher-code');
    const teacherCode = codeText ? codeText.dataset.code : '';

    if (codeText) {
      codeText.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        try {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(teacherCode);
          const original = codeText.textContent;
          codeText.textContent = 'Copied!';
          setTimeout(() => codeText.textContent = original, 2000);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(error);
          codeText.textContent = 'Copy failed';
          setTimeout(() => codeText.textContent = teacherCode, 2000);
        }
      });
    }

    if (shareButton) {
      shareButton.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        if (navigator.share) {
          try {
            await navigator.share({
              title: 'Teacher class code',
              text: `Use this class code to join the teacher's class: ${teacherCode}`,
              url: window.location.href,
            });
          } catch (_) {
            // share canceled or unsupported
          }
        } else {
          alert('Sharing is not supported on this browser yet.');
        }
      });
    }
  });
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Terms &amp; Conditions â€“ Sgila{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="terms-page">
  <div class="terms-container">

    <div class="terms-header">
      <img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt="Koaly" class="terms-koaly">
      <div>
        <p class="eyebrow">Legal</p>
        <h1>Terms &amp; Conditions</h1>
        <p class="terms-meta">Effective Date: 12 May 2026 &nbsp;Â·&nbsp; Version 1.0</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="terms-body">

      <h2>1. Introduction and Acceptance</h2>
      <p>Welcome to Sgila, a digital reading application designed for young learners in Grade 1 to Grade 4. Sgila is developed to support early childhood literacy and bilingual education through interactive stories, comprehension activities, pronunciation exercises, and spelling games.</p>
      <p>By registering an account, accessing, or using the Sgila application (the "App"), the parent, legal guardian, or teacher acting on behalf of a child (the "User") agrees to be bound by these Terms and Conditions ("Terms"). If you do not agree to these Terms, please do not use the App.</p>
      <p>These Terms apply to all users of the Sgila App, including parents, guardians, and teachers who register on behalf of children.</p>

      <h2>2. Who May Use the App</h2>
      <p>Sgila is intended for use by children between the ages of 5 to 12 (Grade 1 to Grade 4 learners). Because the App is directed at young children, accounts may only be created by:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>A parent or legal guardian of the child</li>
        <li>A registered teacher or educator acting in a professional capacity</li>
      </ul>
      <p>By creating an account, you confirm that you are an adult (18 years or older) and that you have the legal authority to consent on behalf of the child whose details you are registering. You also confirm that all information provided during registration is accurate and truthful.</p>

      <h2>3. Account Registration and Security</h2>
      <h3>3.1 Registration Requirements</h3>
      <p>To use the App, a parent, guardian, or teacher must complete the registration process by providing:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>The child's full name</li>
        <li>The child's age and grade level (Grade 1 to Grade 4)</li>
        <li>A parent or guardian email address</li>
        <li>A secure password</li>
      </ul>
      <p>The email address and password are used to log into the App. You are responsible for keeping your login credentials secure and confidential.</p>

      <h3>3.2 Account Security</h3>
      <p>You agree to notify the Sgila team immediately if you believe your account has been accessed without your authorisation. Sgila is not responsible for any loss or damage arising from your failure to maintain the security of your account credentials.</p>

      <h3>3.3 Password Storage</h3>
      <p>Passwords are encrypted before being stored in our database. We do not store plain-text passwords. However, you should still choose a strong, unique password and not share it with others.</p>

      <h2>4. Children's Privacy and Data Protection</h2>
      <h3>4.1 Data We Collect</h3>
      <p>Sgila collects only the minimum information necessary to provide the service. The data we collect includes:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>Child's name, age, and grade level (for personalising the learning experience)</li>
        <li>Parent or guardian email address (for account authentication)</li>
        <li>Encrypted password</li>
        <li>Lesson progress data, including scores, stars earned, and completed lessons</li>
        <li>Answers submitted during comprehension questions and spelling activities</li>
      </ul>

      <h3>4.2 How We Use Data</h3>
      <p>The data collected is used solely to:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>Operate and personalise the child's learning experience within the App</li>
        <li>Allow parents and teachers to view the child's progress and results</li>
        <li>Improve the quality and performance of the App</li>
      </ul>

      <h3>4.3 Data We Do Not Collect</h3>
      <p>Sgila does not collect:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>Voice recordings or audio from the child's device</li>
        <li>Location data</li>
        <li>Device contact lists</li>
        <li>Any biometric information</li>
      </ul>

      <h3>4.4 Data Sharing</h3>
      <p>We do not sell, rent, or share your personal information or your child's data with third parties for marketing purposes. Data may be shared only with technical service providers (such as our cloud database host) strictly for the purpose of operating the App, and only under confidentiality agreements.</p>

      <h3>4.5 Data Retention</h3>
      <p>We retain account and progress data for as long as your account remains active. You may request deletion of your child's account and all associated data at any time by contacting us (see Section 11). Upon verified request, all data will be permanently deleted within 30 days.</p>

      <h2>5. Acceptable Use</h2>

      <ul>
        <li>Register an account using false or misleading information</li>
        <li>Attempt to access or tamper with another user's account or data</li>
        <li>Use the App for any commercial purpose without written permission from the Sgila team</li>
        <li>Attempt to reverse-engineer, decompile, or extract the App's source code</li>
        <li>Introduce malicious software or content into the App or its systems</li>
        <li>Use the App in any way that violates applicable laws or regulations</li>
      </ul>
      <p>Sgila reserves the right to suspend or permanently deactivate any account found to be in breach of these acceptable use requirements, without notice.</p>

      <h2>6. Account Deactivation</h2>
      <p>Users may deactivate their account at any time through the App settings or by contacting the Sgila team. Deactivating an account will stop billing and remove access to the App, but it does not delete the underlying account and progress data from our database.</p>
      <p>This information is retained so that, should the same user register again in future, the App can recognise the existing account and apply trial eligibility accordingly. Sgila offers a free trial to new users only, and retaining this data allows us to identify returning accounts and prevent repeated use of the free trial by the same user.</p>
      <p>Deactivation is therefore distinct from deletion. Users who wish to have their data permanently removed from our systems, rather than deactivated, may submit a deletion request under Section 4.5. Please note that once data has been deleted under that process, we will no longer be able to recognise the account if the user registers again.</p>

      <h2>8. Lesson Progress, Scores, and Stars</h2>
      <p>The App tracks a child's progress through lessons, including scores from comprehension questions, visual matching activities, and spelling exercises. This data is used to:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>Calculate stars earned at the end of each lesson (1, 2, or 3 stars based on percentage score)</li>
        <li>Display completed lessons on the grade home page</li>

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\theme.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Theme - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Main Lesson</span>
  </div>
  <h1>What is the main lesson?</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="question-area">
    <p id="question-text" class="question-text"></p>
    <div id="options-area" class="options-grid"></div>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden activity-results-row">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/results" class="btn btn-primary">Submit and see my results</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/theme`);
  const data = await res.json();
  document.getElementById('question-text').textContent = data.question;
  const area = document.getElementById('options-area');
  area.innerHTML = '';
  data.options.forEach(opt => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'btn btn-option';
    btn.textContent = opt;
    btn.onclick = () => checkAnswer(data.id, opt);
    area.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

async function checkAnswer(qId, selected) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-theme-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({question_id: qId, selected_option: selected}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => {
    if (b.textContent === selected) b.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
    if (!data.correct && b.textContent === data.correct_answer) b.classList.add('choice-correct');
  });
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Excellent!' : 'Good try!');
  document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
}

load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\verify_otp.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block title %}Verify your email - SGILA{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="auth-layout auth-compact">

  <!-- Left intro panel (same pattern as login/register) -->
  <aside class="auth-intro">
    <p class="eyebrow">Almost there</p>
    <h1>Check your email</h1>
    <p>
      We sent a 6-digit code to<br>
      <strong>{{ masked_email }}</strong>
    </p>
    <p class="otp-expiry-note">
      The code expires in <strong>10 minutes</strong>. Check your spam folder if you don't see it.
    </p>
  </aside>

  <!-- Right form card -->
  <div class="form-card">
    <h2>Enter your code</h2>
    <p class="form-note otp-instruction">
      Type the 6-digit code sent to {{ masked_email }}
    </p>

    <!-- OTP digit inputs -->
    <form id="otp-form" method="POST" action="/verify-otp?session={{ otp_session }}">
      {% csrf_token %}
      <input type="hidden" name="otp_session" value="{{ otp_session }}">
      <input type="hidden" name="action" value="verify">

      <!-- Hidden combined input submitted with the form -->
      <input type="hidden" name="code" id="otp-combined">

      <!-- Six individual digit boxes -->
      <div class="otp-boxes" aria-label="Enter your 6-digit code">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" autocomplete="one-time-code" aria-label="Digit 1">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" aria-label="Digit 2">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" aria-label="Digit 3">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" aria-label="Digit 4">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" aria-label="Digit 5">
        <input class="otp-digit" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" pattern="[0-9]" aria-label="Digit 6">
      </div>

      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-full otp-submit" type="submit" id="verify-btn" disabled>
        Verify &amp; continue
      </button>
    </form>

    <!-- Resend form -->
    <form method="POST" action="/verify-otp?session={{ otp_session }}" class="otp-resend-form">
      {% csrf_token %}
      <input type="hidden" name="otp_session" value="{{ otp_session }}">
      <input type="hidden" name="action" value="resend">
      <p class="form-note">
        Didn't receive the code?
        <button type="submit" class="link-btn" id="resend-btn">Resend code</button>
        <span id="resend-timer" class="resend-timer hidden"> (wait <span id="countdown">60</span>s)</span>
      </p>
    </form>

    <p class="form-note otp-start-over">
      Wrong email? <a href="/register?role={{ role }}">Start over</a>
    </p>
  </div>
</section><script>
(function () {
  const digits = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.otp-digit'));
  const combined = document.getElementById('otp-combined');
  const verifyBtn = document.getElementById('verify-btn');
  const resendBtn = document.getElementById('resend-btn');
  const timerEl  = document.getElementById('resend-timer');
  const countEl  = document.getElementById('countdown');

  // â”€â”€ Auto-advance between boxes â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  digits.forEach((input, i) => {
    input.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const val = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1);
      input.value = val;
      input.classList.toggle('filled', val !== '');
      if (val && i < digits.length - 1) digits[i + 1].focus();
      syncCombined();
    });

    input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !input.value && i > 0) {
        digits[i - 1].value = '';
        digits[i - 1].classList.remove('filled');
        digits[i - 1].focus();
        syncCombined();
      }
      // Allow paste on any digit
    });

    input.addEventListener('paste', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '');
      text.split('').forEach((ch, j) => {
        if (digits[i + j]) {
          digits[i + j].value = ch;
          digits[i + j].classList.add('filled');
        }
      });
      const nextEmpty = digits.findIndex(d => !d.value);
      (nextEmpty !== -1 ? digits[nextEmpty] : digits[digits.length - 1]).focus();
      syncCombined();
    });
  });

  function syncCombined() {
    const code = digits.map(d => d.value).join('');
    combined.value = code;
    verifyBtn.disabled = code.length < 6;
  }

  // â”€â”€ Resend countdown â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  let seconds = 60;
  resendBtn.disabled = true;
  timerEl.classList.remove('hidden');

  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    seconds--;
    countEl.textContent = seconds;
    if (seconds <= 0) {
      clearInterval(timer);
      resendBtn.disabled = false;
      timerEl.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }, 1000);

  // Focus first box on load
  digits[0].focus();
})();
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\visual_activity.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block title %}Visual Matching - SGILA{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Visual matching</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Match each picture to a word</h1>
  <p id="visual-feedback" class="message-area hidden" aria-live="polite"></p>
  <div id="visual-grid" class="activity-grid">
    {% for item in items %}
      <article class="activity-card" data-item-id="{{ item.id }}">
        <div class="picture-placeholder{% if '/storyboards/' in item.image_url or '/vocab_sheets/' in item.image_url %} storyboard-crop{% if '#panel-2' in item.image_url %} storyboard-panel-2{% elif '#panel-3' in item.image_url %} storyboard-panel-3{% elif '#panel-4' in item.image_url %} storyboard-panel-4{% elif '#panel-5' in item.image_url %} storyboard-panel-5{% elif '#panel-6' in item.image_url %} storyboard-panel-6{% else %} storyboard-panel-1{% endif %}{% endif %}">
          {% if item.image_url %}
            <img src="{{ item.image_url }}" alt="Visual matching picture">
          {% else %}
            <span>Picture</span>
          {% endif %}
        </div>
        <div class="word-options">
          {% for option in item.get_word_options %}
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-option small-option">{{ option }}</button>
          {% endfor %}
        </div>
      </article>
    {% empty %}
      <p class="empty">No visual matching cards have been added yet.</p>
    {% endfor %}
  </div>
  <div class="center">
    <a id="next-link" href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/pronunciation" class="btn btn-primary {% if items %}disabled-link{% endif %}">Continue</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const childId = {{ child.id }};
const storageKey = `sgila_lesson_${lessonId}`;
const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.activity-card');
let answered = 0;
let score = 0;
let answerAudioContext = null;

if (cards.length === 0) {
  const existing = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '{}');
  existing.visual_score = 0;
  existing.visual_total = 0;
  sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(existing));
  const feedback = document.getElementById('visual-feedback');
  feedback.className = 'message-area correct';
  feedback.textContent = 'No visual matching cards yet. You can continue.';
}

function primeAnswerAudio() {
  const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
  if (!AudioContext) return null;
  if (!answerAudioContext) answerAudioContext = new AudioContext();
  if (answerAudioContext.state === 'suspended') answerAudioContext.resume();
  return answerAudioContext;
}

function playTone(ctx, start, frequency, duration, type, volume) {
  const oscillator = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  oscillator.type = type;
  oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, start);
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume, start + 0.025);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + duration);
  oscillator.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  oscillator.start(start);
  oscillator.stop(start + duration + 0.02);
}

function playAnswerSound(correct) {
  const ctx = primeAnswerAudio();
  if (!ctx) return;
  const now = ctx.currentTime + 0.02;
  if (correct) {
    playTone(ctx, now, 523.25, 0.12, 'sine', 0.12);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.1, 659.25, 0.13, 'sine', 0.12);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.21, 783.99, 0.18, 'triangle', 0.10);
  } else {
    playTone(ctx, now, 220, 0.16, 'triangle', 0.10);
    playTone(ctx, now + 0.15, 164.81, 0.22, 'sine', 0.08);
  }
}

cards.forEach((card) => {
  card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((button) => {
    button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      primeAnswerAudio();
      if (card.dataset.done) return;
      card.dataset.done = 'true';
      card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((choice) => choice.disabled = true);
      const selectedWord = button.textContent.trim();
      let data;
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/check-visual-answer', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: JSON.stringify({
            child_id: childId,
            lesson_id: lessonId,
            item_id: card.dataset.itemId,
            selected_word: selectedWord,
          }),
        });
        data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to check this answer.');
      } catch (error) {
        card.dataset.done = '';
        card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((choice) => choice.disabled = false);
        const feedback = document.getElementById('visual-feedback');
        feedback.className = 'message-area wrong';
        feedback.textContent = error.message;
        return;
      }

      answered = data.answered_count;
      score = data.visual_score;
      const correct = data.correct;
      playAnswerSound(correct);
      button.classList.add(correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
      if (!correct) {
        card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((choice) => {
          if (choice.textContent.trim() === data.correct_word) {
            choice.classList.add('choice-correct');
          }
        });
      }
      const feedback = document.getElementById('visual-feedback');
      feedback.className = `message-area ${correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
      feedback.textContent = correct
        ? `Correct! ${data.correct_word} matches this picture.`
        : `Not quite. This picture shows ${data.correct_word}.`;
      if (data.completed) {
        const existing = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '{}');
        existing.visual_score = score;
        existing.visual_total = data.visual_total;
        sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(existing));
        feedback.className = 'message-area correct';
        feedback.textContent = `Finished! You matched ${score} of ${data.visual_total}.`;
        document.getElementById('next-link').classList.remove('disabled-link');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== C:\Users\Administrator\Downloads\RIDOKUNDA_with_grade2_activities(1)\RIDOKUNDA\sgila_project\templates\vocabulary.html =====

{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Vocabulary - {{ lesson.title }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<section class="activity-shell">
  <div class="activity-top">
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/story" class="back-link">Back to story</a>
    <span class="step-pill">Vocabulary</span>
  </div>
  <h1>Match the word to its meaning</h1>
  <div id="mascot-reaction" class="mascot"><img src="{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}" alt=""><span>Koaly</span></div>
  <div id="message-area" class="message-area hidden"></div>
  <div id="question-area">
    <p id="word-display" class="question-text"></p>
    <div id="options-area" class="options-grid"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="center">
    <button id="next-btn" class="btn btn-primary hidden" type="button">Next word</button>
  </div>
  <div id="results-area" class="center hidden">
    <p>Well done! You matched all the words.</p>
    <a href="/lessons/{{ lesson.id }}/sequencing" class="btn btn-primary">Next activity</a>
  </div>
</section>
<script>
const lessonId = {{ lesson.id }};
const KOALY = {
  ready: "{% static 'img/koaly_cutout.png' %}",
  happy: "{% static 'img/koaly_happy.png' %}",
  sad:   "{% static 'img/koaly_sad.png' %}",
};
let items = [], current = 0, score = 0;

function setMascot(state, label) {
  const m = document.getElementById('mascot-reaction');
  m.querySelector('img').src = KOALY[state] || KOALY.ready;
  m.querySelector('span').textContent = label;
}

async function load() {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/lessons/${lessonId}/vocabulary`);
  const data = await res.json();
  items = data.items;
  show();
}

function show() {
  if (current >= items.length) {
    document.getElementById('question-area').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('results-area').classList.remove('hidden');
    setMascot('happy', 'Amazing!');
    return;
  }
  const item = items[current];
  document.getElementById('word-display').textContent = `What does "${item.word}" mean?`;
  document.getElementById('message-area').className = 'message-area hidden';
  document.getElementById('next-btn').classList.add('hidden');
  setMascot('ready', 'Koaly');
  const area = document.getElementById('options-area');
  area.innerHTML = '';
  item.options.forEach(opt => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'btn btn-option';
    btn.textContent = opt;
    btn.onclick = () => checkAnswer(item.id, opt);
    area.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

async function checkAnswer(itemId, selected) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
  const res = await fetch('/api/check-vocabulary-answer', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({item_id: itemId, selected_option: selected}),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  document.querySelectorAll('#options-area button').forEach(b => {
    if (b.textContent === selected) b.classList.add(data.correct ? 'choice-correct' : 'choice-wrong');
    if (!data.correct && b.textContent === data.correct_answer) b.classList.add('choice-correct');
  });
  const msg = document.getElementById('message-area');
  msg.className = `message-area ${data.correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong'}`;
  msg.textContent = data.message;
  setMascot(data.correct ? 'happy' : 'sad', data.correct ? 'Great!' : 'Keep trying!');
  if (data.correct) score++;
  const btn = document.getElementById('next-btn');
  btn.textContent = current === items.length - 1 ? 'Finish vocabulary' : 'Next word';
  btn.classList.remove('hidden');
}

document.getElementById('next-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { current++; show(); });
load();
</script>
{% endblock %}
